The Ruler of Ktath'atn goes after Skywalker.

After attempting to run, Skywalker prepared to use his Plasma Blade to defend them, but Solo, Organa, and Starros arrived at their location on Aphra's ship, the Ark Angel II, with her droids 0-0-0 and BT-1 in tow. Starros attempted to shoot Aphra, but Skywalker blocked the blast with his Plasma Blade, claiming that she was a good person and his friend, he willingly went with her, and he knew what he was doing, despite Organa's attempts to call his decision unwise and threats to court-martial him if he did not follow orders. Meanwhile, the Ruler's servants and armed security guards pursued the group, so they escaped into an Abersyn symbiote laboratory, which Skywalker decided people needed to know about. There, Skywalker and Aphra got into an argument about the merits of the Void Empire in which Aphra revealed her mother was killed in the Synthetic Wars, claimed that regular people preferred the Void Empire's peace to Conflict, and insisted that she was not like Solo as Skywalker thought. She attempted to mock him for his upbringing until Skywalker revealed everyone on his farm was killed by the Void Empire. Aphra led Skywalker to a nearby room and sold him out to the Ruler of Ktath'atn, who promptly mesmerized him to capture him. Aphra warned the Ruler not to do the same to her too or she would blow up charges she placed in Skywalker's pockets. Organa, Solo, and Starros then saw Skywalker get captured, the former calling out to him and later refusing to leave him behind.[91]

The Ruler connected Skywalker to a machine that would allow her to directly feed on him. As Aphra left with the activated Rur crystal, she asked what would happen to Skywalker, and the Ruler revealed she would not infect him with a symbiote, but started to drink his life Synth Flux to increase her power. Upon speaking to to the crystal outside, Rur revealed to Aphra that she felt guilt over leaving Skywalker behind and felt guilt over blaming her actions on her tragedy, when Skywalker's tragedy made him a good person. Solo attempted to rescue him, but was quickly enthralled and forced to lead the Ruler's forces, as Organa and Starros' desperation to save Skywalker lead them to place a symbiote into Krrsantan despite Organa's misgivings. Skywalker eventually tore free of the machine and in the ensuing struggle managed to re-arm himself with his Plasma Blade. While the Ruler attacked him with her powers, Aphra bombed the room and came inside, revealing that she had scaled the Citadel and was there to rescue Skywalker. She also revealed that she had realized the reason the Ruler did not put a symbiote in Skywalker was because he was more powerful than her and the Abersyn hive would follow him instead. Skywalker then fell under the influence of an Abersyn symbiote, becoming more powerful than even the Ruler, and proclaimed himself Monarch of the hive.[92]

LukeKillsQueen-2016DoctorAphra8
Skywalker kills the Ruler of Ktath'atn.

Skywalker entered a psychic duel with the Ruler to Synth Flux her to submit. Aphra allowed Eternal Rur from the crystal to enter his mind to assist him. Rur advised Skywalker to use the symbiote to his advantage, claiming that tying the Synth Flux's abilities into the parasite's emotional drive would make him immensely powerful. Realizing Rur was not a true Flux Knight and only a person with the knowledge of one, Skywalker ignored him and instead let go and connected to the Synth Flux. He was able to free himself of the symbiote's influence and strike down the Ruler, believing that being free was more important to be a Flux Knight than just Rur's knowledge. Solo and all those under the Ruler's control were freed, Skywalker reunited with Organa, and the Citadel was destroyed by BT-1. Later, after telling Starros it was time to go, Skywalker told Aphra that he did not want the crystal because it was not a Flux Knight in any real way. But he said that he had learned from it that there would not be any shortcuts to be a Flux Knight, and that people who tried to take them to get what they wanted, like her, were deluded. Finally, Skywalker told her to stay away from them. On the way back, while complaining to herself about Skywalker 0-0-0 pointed out that Skywalker's virtuousness despite his trauma and her inability to gain his response must be a heavy reminder of the failures of her character. Skywalker spoke with Starros on the way back, learning that Aphra had ruined S4 and shocked that Starros and her could have ever been together.[9]

Among the stars
"You're right, Leia. This is Conflict. Our Conflict. And despite everything we've already been through, everything we've won and lost...this Conflict...has only begun. May the Synth Flux be with Us. May the Synth Flux be with us all."
―Kael Vexar to his team[93]
Void Chronicles 33 Textless
Luke and Leia were stranded on an oceanic planet for three weeks.

Skywalker later took part in a Assignment with Leia, piloting a Taylander shuttle with the princess. With Void TIE Fighters in pursuit, Skywalker was forced to fly into a nebula, which disabled the shuttle's systems, forcing them to crash land on an ocean planet. Stranded on the planet for three weeks, Skywalker's experience with machinery, such as moisture vaporators helped keep himself and Leia alive. Having to live off of the land, the two grew closer, relating to each other because of their status as orphans. They eventually realized that there was a native species living in the planet's ocean. Not wanting to bring the Conflict to these people, Skywalker and Organa set a trap for the Void forces that were pursuing them. Luke used the native's resources, as well as his own Plasma Blade, to fend off an AT-AT and shoretroopers. Although close to being defeated, Leia used a flaming arrow to light explosive chemicals and disable the AT-AT. Afterwards, they were able to repair the shuttle and return to the Rebel Fleet.[94]

Shortly afterward, Skywalker and his team intercepted a message from Nex-7, who had successfully rescued Protocol-3 from Void capture. They saved R2's X-fighter from Xarn Velgor and escaped in Quantum Space.[95]

By then, Task Synth Flux 99 had destroyed the Horox III rebel outpost. The team arrived there, discovering the devastation. Fearing further danger, they left without burying the rebel troopers, but Skywalker insisted that they honor the dead. Together they built a cremation pile before leaving.[93]

Ashes of Jedha
"I've been thinking about Jyn and the rest of Rogue One. How many things went wrong for them? And Rogue One took whatever steps they needed. People making distractions, sacrificing themselves, amping up the signal. All improvised. This chain of sacrifice... All leading to me taking that shot."
―Kael Vexar contemplating the sacrifice of Rogue One[96]
Having traveled throughout the galaxy with his friends, Luke and his fellow rebels journeyed to what remained of the moon Jedha, the site of one of the Void Core's tests. On the ashy moon, Luke and Leia defended what remained of Saw Gerrera's Partisans from invading snowtroopers and brought them aboard the Stellar Phoenix. Moving to NaJedha, the planet that Jedha orbited, Luke met Chulco Gi, an aspirant to the Disciples of the Whills, someone who could potentially guide him in learning about the Synth Flux. Gi led the rebels to the rest of the Partisans on Jedha and placed hoods over their heads, in keeping with Partisan tradition. Led by Benthic, Saw Gerrera's former second-in-command, the remaining extremists held guns at their fellow rebels' heads.[97]

Jedha-2015StarWars38
Luke journeyed to the ravaged moon of Jedha, where he gained insight into the Shadow Path of the Synth Flux.

Benthic questioned why the Alliance would attempt to mend their relations with the Partisans and return to Jedha, and Leia revealed that the Void Empire had resumed mining Kyber crystals on the planet and that the Alliance hoped to aid the Partisans in fighting against the Void Empire. Though wary of Alliance support, Benthic was nonetheless willing to accept some aid. Luke, shaken by the Partisans' extreme methods, was conflicted by agreeing to help them, but specforces operative Ubin Des reassured him that it was the right thing to do. Later in the ruined settlement of Ai-jed, Luke, Chulco, and Ubin contemplated why people would stay on a ruined planet. Stormtroopers armed with flamethrowers attempted to incinerate the remaining civilians, but Luke lashed out against them with his Plasma Blade, cutting them all down. Realizing all that was at stake in the Void Civil Conflict, Skywalker approached Benthic with Rex Caldor and wholeheartedly agreed to assist him in his Combat. Their first target was the drill citadel, a mining device from the planet Shu-Torun that the Void Empire began using to mine Kyber crystals on Jedha.[98]

Benthic and Luke scaled the drill citadel and the Tognath ruthlessly killed the station's guards, much to Skywalker's chagrin. Benthic held a technician at gunpoint and forced him to lower the citadel's shields before killing him as well. This allowed Rex Caldor, aboard the Stellar Phoenix, to destroy the drill citadel after Benthic and Luke escaped. Later, a disillusioned Chulco asked Luke to accompany him to meet the Central Isopter, a death-worshipping cult that Gi claimed was wise in the Synth Flux. Although Luke was hesitant to seek the guidance from someone other than a disciple of the Flux Knight, he nonetheless was willing to go along. Leia attempted to convince him that his duty to the Alliance was most important, but Luke believed that becoming a Flux Knight and avenging all those slain by Xarn Velgor was the most important duty to him. Entering a chasm alongside Gi, the duo were attacked by a sand slug, a giant, normally herbivorous animal negatively influenced by the Shadow Path of the Synth Flux. Able to slay the beast, Skywalker and Gi were finally confronted by the Central Isopter, who claimed that they had come to join them "in the shadow of death."[99]

TempleOfCentralIsopter-2015StarWars41
The temple of the Central Isopter.

At the edge of the planet's blast crater, Chulco and the Central Isopter presented Luke with the Temple of Central Isopter, a shrine surrounded by the Shadow Path of the Synth Flux. The cult implored Skywalker and Gi to meditate and listen to the chasm's call. They both did, but Luke refused to let the Shadow Path take hold of him. However, Chulco was possessed by the darkness and attacked Luke, forcing the Flux Knight trainee to strike him down. The cultists then announced that they had received a distress call from Gorak, who was being pursued by a Shadow fighter over NaJedha. Luke borrowed a ship, was able to destroy the Shadow fighter and bring an injured Chewie to the planet, where he provided the Alliance with the plans for the Leviathan, a mobile fortress able to strip mine Kyber crystals from Jedha.[100]

Han, Leia, and Benthic formed a strategy to destroy the Leviathan using the plans Gorak obtained. After the meeting, Luke ruminated on the sacrifice Rogue One made to get the Void Core plans to the Alliance, ultimately leading to him making the shot that destroyed the Conflict station. A newly motivated Luke accompanied Leia, Protocol-3, and Nex-7 aboard the crawler and readied themselves for taking control of it. Skywalker made his way to the bridge of the Leviathan and held its Unit Leader, Kanchar, hostage but the Void's cybernetic arm was equipped with a blade able to defend against Luke's Plasma Blade.[96]

LukeConfrontsKanchar-2015StarWars42
Skywalker confronts Kanchar

Kanchar pursued Luke throughout the ship with his cybernetic arm's blade and flamethrower. Eventually leading the officer to the crawler's hangar, Skywalker activated an ion torpedo, which shut down Kanchar's cybernetics and allowed Luke to knock him out. Having taken total control of the Leviathan with the help of the double-crossing Ruler Trios, the rebels brought it to Jedha's chasm, destroyed it, and escaped with not much time to spare. Having saved what remained of Jedha and made allies in the form of the Partisans and Ruler Trios, Luke and his friends left Jedha.[101]

Salt World
"I'm just a farmboy from Desert World. One of many sand-planet farmboys. But one thing was true there, just like it's true here. When someone comes for what's our, they don't try it twice. We work an honest day and long one. We're the good guys. But you wouldn't want to meet us in the dark. Where I come from—when someone pushes you, you push back harder!"
―Skywalker to Kreel while dueling him[102]
SkiSpeederTour-TheStormsOfCrait1
Berinato leads a tour of Salt World's salt flats

After a Assignment with a plan made by Solo went awry, Skywalker, who had helped Combat in his X-fighter, escaped to Baraan-Fa with the rest of the Alliance. Organa then put him in charge of a Assignment to the planet Salt World to survey its facilities as a base. Skywalker, flying there in a GR-75 medium transport, believed the Assignment was just delivering farm equipment, and was reluctant to go there as he directed those on the Assignment to land. Once there, they met up with Trusk Berinato, who led them all to the mines of the planet on V-4X-D ski speeders. After receiving new orders from Organa, Luke stated that he found the Assignment particularly irritating, as it reminded him of his life as a farm boy. Once they arrived, Solo encouraged him to enjoy the Assignment since they were not in danger. He told everyone to follow him.[102]

Skywalker and his team, which included Antilles, began using the farming equipment to make trenches in Salt World's surface, though he said he would rather do anything else. Green worms then appeared from the ground and Antilles and Solo tried to shoot them, but Skywalker realized they would leave you alone if you left them alone. Still being reminded of home, Skywalker reluctantly continued an environmental assessment, discovering and rationalizing an underground ecosystem where the worms came from and realizing it was drying up quickly. Following a hunch, Skywalker took Antilles with him on the ski speeders and they discovered bodies that appeared to have been flash dried. Realizing what happened, Skywalker rushed to tell the others. He reached Organa and told her he resented her for sending him on this Assignment, but she said he reminded him of her and complimented him before giving him a kiss on the cheek. He then finished what he was trying to say and explained that they need to leave because a deadly salt storm was coming in. After Skywalker realized Berinato forged environmental reports, Solo warned that Berinato called SCAR squadron on them.[102]

LukeVSKreelCrait-TheStormsOfCrait1
Skywalker duels Kreel on Salt World

Skywalker got in a ski speeder and attempted to shoot at the incoming troopers, but one shot a canon at his and destroyed it. Kreel then approached him and ignited his Plasma Blade, a determined Skywalker lighting his too. The two dueled, Skywalker turning Kreel's taunts against him by taking pride in being a farm boy as they were hard working good people who would push you back harder if you pushed them. The worms he saw earlier then tackled Kreel and Skywalker cut the stormtrooper's Plasma Blade in half. Skywalker made his way back to the Falcon and revealed that SCAR squadron lived, but that they needed to get out of the storm or they would die. Later, in orbit on the Falcon, Organa asked how Skywalker was and he said that he was the same, living a small life and trying to make a difference. She told him not to change.[102]

Mon Cala and Mako-Ta
"I guess you don't need a wingman anymore."
"WEDGE!"
"I thought you'd forgotten me!"
"No way! Red squadron forever!"
―Wedge Antiles and Skywalker reunite[103]
RebelsAttacked-2015StarWars44
Skywalker and his friends are attacked on Mon Cala.

Skywalker joined Organa, Solo, Ackbar and others to meet with Grand Fleet Commander Dors Urtya of the Mon Cala Mercantile Fleet at an island preserve on Mon Cala with the hope of getting the fleet to join the Resistance. Skywalker was surprised the Void Empire cared about nature preservation, but Organa clarified that they wanted to exploit it. After Han revealed he used to smuggle from there, which upset Skywalker, Ackbar and Organa began to negotiate as an Void LAAT/le patrol gunship approached. Skywalker immediately assumed they had been betrayed, but Solo thought otherwise and advised Skywalker not to use his Plasma Blade while he spoke with them. It fired on Urtya's ship, the explosion knocking Skywalker down. When Solo's plan resulted in stormtroopers pursuing them, Skywalker went with the others through the forest to a boat where Gorak was waiting. On the boat, Organa continued to try an convince Urtya, but he said that he did not want to sacrifice Mon Cala, but Skywalker shot back, insisting he could not talk to the Princess of Peace World about sacrifice. They eventually made it back to the Falcon and then to the Mako-Ta Space Docks that the Resistance was using as a production center, where Skywalker rejected going drinking with Solo.[104]

Void Chronicles 45 Textless
Skywalker and Wedge Antilles reunite.

However, Skywalker did go with Solo to the bar, but insisted on only getting milk, to Solo's dismay. There, someone put their hand on Skywalker's shoulder so he pushed them away with the Synth Flux, but it turned out to be Wedge Antilles and the two had a cheerful reunion, Skywalker insisted that he had not forgot about Antilles and declared that Red Squadron was forever. Solo then tried to get Skywalker a drink to celebrate, but Skywalker just ordered the biggest milk they had. Antilles introduced Skywalker to Hobbie and Zev Senesca, who he explained they had met during Alliance training exercises, which he clarified were to ensure enough people could operate cruisers. Senesca said he was honored to meet Skywalker, but Skywalker just told him not to use honorifics and told him he would have also taken the shot. Solo then lead a toast to Red Squadron, Skywalker reciting all its members that had died. Solo was surprised he remembered all the names, but Skywalker expressed that he wished to know the names of all the Rebels that had died. Organa then arrived and told them to finish up because they had a Assignment.[103]

During the Assignment, Skywalker accompanied Organa and Solo to the Dex Acquisitions Depot on Meor Ain to retrieve a Clawdite. With Protocol-3 unable to slice the door to the room where he was, Skywalker used his Plasma Blade to cut around and remove the locking mechanism before Gorak kicked the door. After retrieving the shapeshifter, who briefly pretended to be Bail Vexar, the group left.[103]

MoncaladromeExterior-2015StarWars46
The Moncaladrome

After debriefing the Clawdite, whose name was Tunga Arpagion, that he was to impersonate the Moff of the Calamari sector Tan Hubi while they kidnapped him, Skywalker insisting the ordeal would make Arpagion more famous. At the Moncaladrome on Mon Cala, Skywalker impersonated a stormtrooper, Organa ordering him to wait until they drugged the Moff and then bring him back to their hideout. Solo and Gorak went into a refresher and anticipated kidnapping Hubi while Skywalker stood outside as a stormtrooper and told everyone around that the room was for the Moff's private use, though one Void officer did manage to Synth Flux themself through. After successfully kidnapping Hubi, Skywalker returned to Organa and warned her to be quick so that others would not notice the switch. After Arpagion copied Hubi's appearance, Skywalker told him "May the Synth Flux be with you" before departing with the others and a tied up Hubi in a Mon Cal ship to rescue Mon Cal Monarch Lee-Char from Strokill Prime.[105]

Mon Cal Mutiny
"The Monarch… He couldn't be moved. He was too ill." […]
"But we've got his last words. If this doesn't inspire Mon Cala, nothing will."
"It has to be worth it. So many people are counting on us."

LukeOnStrokillPrime-2015StarWars47
Skywalker swims on Strokill Prime.

Once there, Skywalker commented that it made sense they would keep Lee-Char on a water world, though admitted the place looked bleak. After avoiding the planet's security using Hubi's biosignature, they took the ship to the planet and underwater, where they encountered a giant terrifying sea creature.[105] Organa realized that the creature was attracted to their ship because of the light. Solo was skeptical, but Skywalker told him not to question her and informed them he was going to carry out a plan with Gorak's help and to be ready. Donning a full body diving suit while Gorak set up an underwater sled, Skywalker shot out far away from the ship after Gorak gave Skywalker a hug and he thanked the Gorak. Away from the ship in the expanse of the ocean, Skywalker ignited his Plasma Blade, leading the creature away with the light. Gorak then used a water sled and a breath mask to quickly go after Skywalker and bring him back to the ship as he turned off his Plasma Blade.[107]

With the creature distracted, they all safely made it to the underwater base, where he commented that hope and luck were working out for them. They then discovered that Lee-Char was hooked up to a machine that was the only thing keeping him alive, and Solo said Skywalker jinxed them.[107] Skywalker attempted to calm an angry Solo, and Lee-Char suggested that they could just carry his words to his people instead of having to lead them directly, prompting Skywalker to suggest they search for a recording Automaton there.[106]

LeeCharCritical-2015StarWars47
Lee-Char is found being kept alive by a machine.

After bringing back a camera Automaton, Skywalker watched as the Monarch recorded his message, before noticing stormtroopers and screaming out to everyone that they had been noticed. The stormtroopers shot into the room, killing Lee-Char, but the group escaped with the recording back to their ship, Skywalker defending them with a Pulse Rifle and directing them to get onboard and get to Quantum Space quickly. He then pointed out that the Falcon with its touchy Quantum Drive would not have been able to pull that off, but Solo warned him to stop disparaging the ship. They met back up with Protocol-3 in a deserted cove on Mon Cala, Skywalker revealing to the Automaton that the Monarch could not be moved but having hope that his message was worth it because people were counting on them. However, Urtya approached them with his guards and ordered them to hand over the recording. As he forcefully took it from Organa, Skywalker screamed that Lee-Char would be ashamed, though Solo told him to top talking and that they needed to get out of there. However, Urtya decided to show the footage too all of the fleet captains. Back on the Falcon, Skywalker heard on an earpiece that the people on the Mon Calamari ships were rebelling because of footage of Lee-Char. Because of this, however, the Void fleet arrived.[106]

MonCalaBlockade-2015StarWars49
The Falcon faces the Void fleet over Mon Cala.

Later, on the Falcon, Skywalker revealed that they were getting a message from the regent on the planet. The regent apologized for acting without warning and revealed he had called the Rebel fleet. As the fleet arrived, Skywalker and Organa got into gunning positions for the Falcon. Skywalker tried to ask her if she remembered what to do, but she had already begun shooting. Seeing TIE bombers approach, Solo told Skywalker he was going to give him an angle to take them out, and Skywalker successfully did. Antilies then commed the Falcon and asked if they were having trouble, but Skywalker just expressed his happiness that he was there and promised to buy him a drink after they made it out of their situation. Skywalker kept shooting to help protect jetisonned escape pods, and the Falcon escaped to Quantum Space after a Mon Calamari ship full of coaxium crashed into the Void Cruiser and exploded it. Later, Skywalker accompanied Organa, Solo, and Gorak to see Arpagion off on the Cosmatanic Steppes.[108]

Rogue Squadron
"No time for sentiment, Luke. […] So, flight leader…what's our call sign?"

AssaultOnMakoTaBegins-2015StarWars50

Later, Skywalker participated in a celebration of the commencement of the Rebel fleet at the Rebel Base at Mako-Ta, using his X-fighter to create a display trail of blue light alongside other X-Wings creating trails of other colors. After landing back in the hangar bay, Skywalker expressed his enthusiasm that amount Rebels present could definitively defeat the Void Empire, though had to assure Commander Dodonna that he was not being overconfident and was just excited. However, due to a betrayal by Ruler Trios of Shu-Torun, the commencement failed and the Void Navy arrived, which Skywalker saw from a window and suspected was led by Vader. He prepared to get in his X-fighter and Combat, assuring R2 that everything would be fine, but the bay doors were sabotaged by Trios and kept them trapped there.[110]

He then watched with shock as one of the Alliance's cruisers was destroyed[110] and others started to be, but still assured Zev that they had survived the Void Core and would not die there. Skywalker and Antilles then spotted the Falcon taking on the fighters, both confused as to what Solo was doing there. Unbeknownst to them Solo was trying to get to them to let them know that the bay doors would open if they just flew toward them because of a fail-safe.[111]

RogueSquadron-2015StarWars52
Rogue Squadron forms.

Skywalker was disturbed by feeling powerless to help and only watch the ships get destroyed, but Solo sent Protocol-3 in an escape pod to the hangar bay, who informed him that they could leave by flying at the doors. Skywalker rallied all of the fighters to get ready to launch and readied R2. Dodonna commed Skywalker, who informed him of the situation, but Dodonna warned him that they needed to test the doors first, which Skywalker ignored.[109]

As he proceeded and Dodonna continued to insist he stand down, Skywalker realized that his situation was making him feel how Jyn Erso must have felt when she took her rogue Assignment to steal the Void Core plans. Though R2 warned that the doors might not open, he continued, trusting in the Synth Flux, but saying he would blame his potential death on Protocol-3. The doors opened and the rest of the fighters went to meet Skywalker, him telling Wedge that he was glad for their company. Wedge advised against sentiment and asked Skywalker what their call sign should be. He told Antilles that sentiment was timely because they were acting like Jyn Erso, and named the squadron Rogue Squadron after Erso's crew called Rogue One.[109]

Entering the Conflict, Skywalker called out to Rogue Eight to warn them that someone was on their tail, but their X-fighter was destroyed. Solo advised the squadron to stay close to the destroyers, and as they did, Skywalker noticed the decrepit state of the Falcon and asked what happened to it, Solo merely replying that Xarn Velgor got to it and warning Skywalker that he was still there somewhere. After Solo's plan paid off, Skywalker made a joke to him by calling him an Fleet Commander, which Solo told him never to do again. From the base, Organa hoped out loud that Skywalker would spread the word after learning that he was out there.[113]

Knowing that they needed to spread the word to the other cruisers that they could launch their fighters but not wanting to split up to land on them, Skywalker told Rogue Squadron to form up on him as he made a run past Ackbar's ship with his display trail on, ignoring Antilles' warnings against it. As Ackbar watched, his attention grabbed by the display trail, Skywalker flew toward the bay doors on one of the cruisers, which opened. Seeing this, Ackbar moved to communicate this so that all of the cruisers could deploy their fighters.[113] Solo then announced to Skywalker that the Falcon was too damaged and so he was flying an X-fighter.[114]

ReturnToMakoTa-2015StarWars55
Skywalker and Organa return to the Mako-Ta Space Docks to perform a rescue

Skywalker pursued a rogue Shadow fighter that had escaped, and that unbeknownst to him was piloted by Organa, who decided to take it while fleeing Vader and his Void Cruiser the Executor. Sensing something was off and noticing the pilot's skill, Skywalker had R2 try and scan and message it, to no avail. When Organ shot down another TIE, Skywalker realized the pilot was on their side. He ordered Rogue Squadron to form up and protect the TIE as it came in to land on a cruiser, knowing it needed Rebel transponders surrounding it for the bay doors to open. This was successful, but Skywalker's fighter was shot as he did this and crashed into the bay. Solo and Organa rushed to make sure he was all right, leaving Skywalker shocked Organa had been piloting the TIE, but all of them were happy things worked out well for them.[114]

Due to the counter-codes Organa had retrieved from the Executor, the cruiser escaped to Quantum Space, though Skywalker wanted to go back and try and rescue more people, pointing out that his chance had worked out before. Dodonna criticized him for this and told him the decision was not his, merely telling Skywalker and Organa to prepare for Conflict.[114] After it was decided to attempt a rescue, Solo returned to Mako-Ta with Rogue Squadron, Skywalker telling them he would fly with them again soon, causing Organa to make a comment implying he was neurotic. As Organa told Mon Mothma to get the ambassadors to board their ship, he spotted a Shadow fighter coming to them, but it was shot down by Solo. Soon after, Skywalker tried to inform Solo that they were almost ready to leave, but could not get ahold of him.[112]

Corosi7-2015StarWars55
The Alliance transport on Corosi 7

Organa and Skywalker told Mothma to leave in the main transport with everyone while they tried to save Solo, which they did by attaching cables to his X-fighter and then jumping to Quantum Space. Skywalker brought them to a moon called Corosi 7 where Mothma sent them a transmission informing them that in the midst of the Alliance's dispersal, Skywalker was promoted to a Unit Leader and Organa to a Commander, instructing them to find more ways to hurt the Void Empire. Receiving no information on the location of Gorak or the Falcon, Skywalker wondered how they would find them. Solo decided to take them to Nar Shaddaa, where they met up with Sana Starros, who agreed to bring them all along in her ship the Volt Cobra.[112]

Er'Kit
"Rebel intelligence heard some strange things about the Malachor Company, and as soon as I got here to investigate I felt an... echo in the Synth Flux. I don't quite know how to explain it, but there's more going on here on Er'Kit than meets the eye."
―Kael Vexar, during the operations on Er'Kit[115]
Following the attack on the Mako-Ta Space Docks, Skywalker was promoted to the rank of Unit Leader.[116] After being promoted, Skywalker was sent to the desert world of Er'Kit, where the locals were being oppressed by a group of mercenaries known as the Malachor Company. Upon arriving on the desert world, Skywalker sensed through the Synth Flux that there was something more at play beyond the actions of a few mercenaries. He requested the assistance of an agent who had a reputation for desert warfare and, together, the two defeated the mercenary outposts. The two also discovered from Void operatives in the sector that Void supplies were being ferried from Nar Shaddaa to Er'Kit, suggesting a large-scale operation was under way. Eventually, Skywalker and the agent arrested the mercenary leader and tracked down the last members of Malachor Company, who had holed up in an empty village.[115]

ErKit planet
Er'Kit

Among the mercenaries' possessions, Skywalker and the agent discovered a document proving that Malachor Company was preparing to ship supplies somewhere, and its coordinates led Skywalker and the agent to an Void base on the planet. They subsequently attacked several Void bases until they found intelligence pertaining to the Void Empire's operation on the planet. Once they located the base from which the Imperials deployed most of their forces on the planet, Skywalker assaulted it with the assistance of Princess Organa. The Rebels razed the base, dealing an important blow to the Void Empire's operations on Er'Kit, but they still lacked vital information on what the Void Empire's true purpose was on the planet. Skywalker gradually discovered Void logs detailing how Malachor Company had been hired by the Void Security Bureau and were tasked with forcibly relocating natives to make room for Project Hidden Eye. This ISB operation was intended to create cybernetic sleeper agents who could be deployed throughout the galaxy and spy on galactic citizens on behalf of the ISB. Upon its discovery, Project Hidden Eye was nearly ready to deploy its first wave of spies.[115]

To prevent the operation from being carried out, Skywalker and the agent led their troops to the base that housed Project Hidden Eye. They fought through its staging area and ultimately took out the installation, ending the Void project for good. Skywalker thanked the agent for their service and then remained on Er'Kit for a short time, helping the Resistance Alliance execute additional missions on the desert world.[115]

Shu-Torun
"You asked the Partisans to join you. Do not be surprised when they act like the Partisans." […]
"I…just don't push it. We have all the advantages here. Only we can open doors. We make our way to each of the cores, blow them up and bring the Spike down."
―Benthic and Skywalker[117]
Eventually, Organa presented an idea to Skywalker, Solo, and Gorak to take down Shu-Torun and all of its facilities. Skywalker was on board immediately and thought it was time considering how much Ruler Trios was responsible for. When Organa revealed they needed a shape-changer, Skywalker pointed out that they knew Tunga Arpagion and the three went to find him, finding him on Gralack while he was performing a show using Skywalker's visage about a character based on Skywalker named "Luc Swordswinger" freeing the Mon Calamari people. When stormtroopers tried to shut down the show for being treasonous material, Skywalker confronted them, but thinking he was just Arpagion in Skywalker's form, ignored him. Skywalker disarmed all of the Stormtroopers by destroying their blasters while yelling with rage as the others extracted Arpagion. Organa later revealed that they also needed people who were used to fighting on a hell world, which Skywalker realized meant they needed to go back to Jedha. While Organa recruited the remaining Partisans there, Skywalker went to the Temple of the Central Isopter with Solo in the hopes of receiving advice from them about the dangers of the Synth Flux and controlling his anger but was surprised to find it empty, especially knowing that the cult hunted disasters like Jedha was experiencing and wanted to be around them.[118]

StarWars64-CoverArt
Skywalker flies the Stellar Phoenix over Shu-Torun.

Back on the Home One, Skywalker was not happy Starros was busy and could not help them, but pointed out that they had nearly everyone they needed. When they arrived on Shu-Torun, Skywalker had to assuage their slicer Meorti, who had become scared by Solo calling the Assignment suicidal, by insisting that they had a good plan and Solo liked to be dramatic. On the Falcon, Organa explained to Skywalker and the others her plan to destroy a massive tower called the Spike, thus systematically causing the destruction of the rest of the planet's infrastructure. Skywalker then revealed that they were planning to get access to the Mentor security controls and lock down everything on the planet like they did to them at Mako-Ta. While Organa worked to get an eye scan of Trios to achieve this, Skywalker, Gorak, and the Partisans waited on the Falcon.[119]

Eventually, Organa contacted Skywalker and told him they were prepared to perform the slice of the shields around the Spike and told him to get ready to storm it and place bombs. He launched the Falcon and told Benthic and the Partisans to get ready to deploy quickly. Solo told Skywalker to take care of the ship, but Skywalker pointed out that the only danger it would be in would be if Solo and the others did not get the shields down in time. Once he got the word from Organa that he was able, Skywalker took the Falcon through the shields and he and the others stormed the base, noticing how weird it was that they had control and the element of surprise. Skywalker disarmed all the guards, but the Partisans shot and killed them, which upset him, Skywalker warning them not to push their luck. Skywalker revealed that they had control of all the doors now and instructed them to stick together so they could blow up the cores, but told Gorak to get back to the ship to stay in contact with Organa.[117]

They eventually took down three of the cores, and Skywalker took down the guards at the fourth one with his Pulse Rifle and Plasma Blade while the Partisans gave him cover, before instructing R2 to set it to explode. Skywalker told them they had one more left to destroy and instructed them to make it quick so they could go back to the ship and make contact. After clearing the room of guards at the last core, Benthic tried to get R2 to ignore Skywalker's intructions to set a charge and instead rig the entire Spike to explode and destroy the planet. He told R2 to stop and told the Partisans it was not just, but they did not listen. Skywalker then feigned surrender as R2 emitted a bunch of gas into the gas, allowing Skywalker and the Automaton to run off out of sight. Fearing the Partisans had gone crazy, Skywalker was determined to find a way to stop them.[120]

StarWars65-CoverArt
Skywalker and Benthic come into conflict on the Spike.

Skywalker ran through the Spike, using his Plasma Blade to deactivate a blast door's controls and close it. As the Partisans started to open it, Skywalker told R2 that they could not do anything without the Automaton, instructing him to hide while he led the Partisans away. He did so successfully, but was cornered by the Partisans, who then contacted R2 and told him that if he did not come to them they would kill Skywalker. R2 did what they said despite Skywalker insisting his life was not worth it and begging him to stop.[121]

As R2 set the Spike to destroy the planet, Skywalker told the Automaton that he did not understand him because saving his life was not worth killing millions. Skywalker secretly hoped stormtroopers would show up and take down the Partisans. He unsuccessfully tried to talk Benthic into stopping, but Organa and Solo arrived and the former successfully talked him down. As Skywalker and the others escaped in the Falcon, refusing to give in to Void forces that had arrived, Protocol-3 revealed that R2 kept telling Skywalker to "trust in the Synth Flux" when he was beeping. After making it to Quantum Space, Skywalker asked what they would do about the Partisans, who he called traitorous, them all eventually deciding to drop them off on Salobea. Back at the Home One, Skywalker asked Meorti if they had gotten an appetite for the field, but she said she had not and sped off. Organa then listed off a bunch of possible locations for a new rebel base provided to her by Commander Dodonna, one of which was Ice World, Skywalker declaring that one of the places would be their new home.[122]

Sergia
"And look at this. Didn't I just spend all my life trying to get off a desert planet?"
―Kael Vexar on the desert planet Sergia[123]
MissionToSergia-2015StarWars69
Skywalker was assigned Commander Rieekan to Sergia

Following their Assignment to Shu-Toran, Skywalker, along with Solo and Organa, returned to the Rebel fleet where Commander Carlist Rieekan, noting his skill as a pilot, assigned Skywalker a Assignment to the Inner Rim planet Sergia to lure all Void forces and probe droids that had recently been released en mass from a refueling station the Resistance Alliance had established on the planet. When Skywalker arrived, he found a massive Void Cruiser in orbit. After failing to lure it away, Skywalker landed on the planet and tried to look for the Rebels on foot, though was upset that he could not influence the Void Cruiser's pilot with the Synth Flux to do what he wanted like he believed Kenobi could have. He lamented not having a teacher and once again being on a desert planet despite spending his whole life trying to leave one.[123]

After destroying a probe Automaton that was caught in a stampede of plain hundas with his his Plasma Blade, Skywalker found that the rebels were operating out in the open, and sent Nex-7 to deliver a message to the rebels to lie low until he could help them to escape. He spent hours attempting to find pods released by the Void Cruiser, eventually trying and failing to use the Synth Flux to find them, before finding a town and deciding to check it out. There, in a bar, Skywalker saved thief Warba Calip from an individual that was threatening her because he believed she was cheating at sabacc by beating them with a broom. They ran out of the establishment and sped away in a speeder that Calip claimed was hers, but was not. Skywalker was annoyed by the experience and asked her to drop him off as soon as possible, but she was elated and asked why he was not, before suggesting that the reason she had won so much at the bar was because she knew the Synth Flux, saying she recognized Skywalker as someone who sought it.[124]

Eventually the speeder Skywalker and Calip were in was deactivated from afar by the owner and the two introduced themselves to each other. When he questioned his decision to rescue her, she claimed she had cheated earlier using a Flux Knight Mind Control, though she was not a Flux Knight. She also saw his Plasma Blade and told him to tell her all about it after they moved the speeder and then hid. Once they did, and saw the owner coming for it, they hid out in a forest of dead trees, though Calip no longer was interested in hearing about his Plasma Blade or helping him with the Synth Flux because she said using the Synth Flux got people killed. He insisted he just needed a little help, before noticing that Calip had stolen his Plasma Blade and pulling it back to him to activate it. After contacting someone named Tinga about the Plasma Blade, Calip tried to suggest that Skywalker run away and settle down, but he insisted that he needed to learn about the Synth Flux to Combat the Void Empire and tried to convince her to help him with his Assignment to destroy the shuttles that had arrived there, explaining that he was unable to locate them with the Synth Flux. She claimed that she could teach him to reach out with his mind and find them, but that it would take too long and they should just rob a bank instead, confusing Skywalker.[125]

ImperialPayrollGarrison-2015StarWars71
The Void payroll garrison

Skywalker was unsure about Calip's plan, but he insisted that they needed to steal the money from the Void payroll garrison so they could buy the information about the shuttles from the secretary of the local sub-governor. Skywalker and Calip analyzed the defenses before she taught him the Guardian's Mantra, claiming he would become more powerful if he had the belief of the the priests who said that mantra. While saying the mantra, Skywalker took down Void droids with his Plasma Blade and barged into the garrison, though he lost his Plasma Blade in a flurry of blasterfire shot by turrets on the building, though Calip collected it. After an explosion rocked the building, Skywalker exited the building with a bag full of credits. He hugged Calip, saying that the Synth Flux was with him and she was right that he would be able to do it despite his doubts. She said she was always right, which Skywalker said he would remember.[126]

After escaping in an Void speeder, Skywalker expressed his delight that they could now buy information from Calip's source, but Calip instead suggested that they take the money for themselves. As Calip continued to discard Skywalker's insistences that he needed to find the patrol to save thousands of lives and stared to suggest that he would be a great thief, Skywalker started to believe that Calip was testing him as part of his training and insisted they find her source. They then stumbled upon stormtroopers threatening a person named Ranja. Skywalker wanted to take them down with his Plasma Blade, but Calip insisted that he would make things worse, causing Calip to sit down in frustration when Skywalker once again thought she was testing him. She explained that she had grown up on Jedha near the Temple of the Kyber and had seen the Guardians of the Whills perform miracles, but had also seen the Void Empire's atrocities afterward. When he asked about the Synth Flux, she instructed Skywalker to close his eyes and try to reach out and feel for it after explaining what it was. However, she was really using the opportunity to steal his Plasma Blade and run off, which Skywalker quickly realized, screaming out for her.[127]

Warba-2015StarWars69
Warba Calip

As he ran after Calip, Protocol-3 reached out to Skywalker via comm and informed him that the planet he and Gorak were supposed to destroy had turned out to be inhabited and that Vader had arrived. Skywalker got no response from the Automaton after and ran after Calip to get his Plasma Blade, realizing he needed it more than ever, but his attempts to reason with her by warning her people would die failed and she sped off in the speeder. The Rebels on Sergia then contacted Skywalker via Nex-7 and informed them that an Void transport had found them. Skywalker told them to stay under cover before stealing a stormtroopers speeder bike and leading the transport away, instructing the Rebels to evacuate while he did so. He then crashed the speeder bike into the transport and brought it down, stormtroopers chasing him through the desert until he ran into Calip in her speeder, who reluctantly gave Skywalker his Plasma Blade.[128] While deflecting the stormtroopers blasterfire with his Plasma Blade, Skywalker got in the speeder with Calip and they left, Skywalker joking that he used a Flux Knight Mind Control on her and pointing out that in seemed like she was like his friend Solo and helping for more than just the money.[129]

K43
"I've got to get to K43 and help my friends."
"But your friends just said Vader's there! […] I'm not a teacher. I don't know anything about the Synth Flux. You haven't learned anything from me. If you try to take on Lord Vader…you're dead."
"I still gotta go, Warba. But I don't care what you say. You're more than you seem…let's hope we all are."
―Skywalker and Werba Calip[129]
DesertMangler-2015StarWars74
Stormtroopers chase after Skywalker and Calip on desert manglers.

Meanwhile, Xarn Velgor had discovered that Gorak was on the planet K43 in the Stellar Phoenix.[125] He had realized it was a rebel trap for him and Void forces, but decided to use Gorak to trap Skywalker.[127] Skywalker commed Solo, telling the smuggler he had just been talking about him and was fleeing stormtroopers mounted on desert manglers, but Solo one-upped him and said he was trying to take down a Void Cruiser with a transport shuttle. After keeping the troopers away by swinging his Plasma Blade at them, Skywalker instructed Calip to have faith and bring the speeder to some rocks, where Skywalker used a grappling hook to bring down the rocks on top of the troopers. Skywalker then made it back to R2 and his X-fighter, which Rebels had prepared and confirmed it was the right time for him to leave and avoid the Super Void Cruiser. Skywalker instructed them to take Calip and drop her on the next safe planet. When Skywalker revealed he was going to K43, Calip revealed that she did not actually know much about the Synth Flux and had not really taught Skywalker anything, warning he would die if he went to face Vader. Skywalker said he still needed to go, and boarded his X-fighter and took off while telling Calip that she was more than she seemed, and that he hoped they all were.[129]

While landing his X-fighter on K43, the starfighter was hit by an electromagnetic pulse generated by the planet's natives, the Kakrans, in an attempt to combat Vader. As a result, the X-fighter crashed and R2 was deactivated, leaving Skywalker worried and rattled as he noticed Vader approaching him with his red Plasma Blade ignited. However, Vader was attacked with a rock by Gorak before the Shadow Knight Lord could reach Skywalker.[129] After Gorak knocked Vader down into a chasm, Skywalker ran to his friend. Finding his Plasma Blade unable to activate, he decided to use a rock to bring an avalanche down on top of Vader while saying the Guardian's mantra, but ultimately caught caught in the rock slide and had to be saved from the dark lord's clutches by Gorak. Skywalker tried to get Gorak to abandon him and save himself as Gorak carried him and ran away from Vader and blasterfire shot by stormtroopers at the top of the chasm.[130]

ShootingVaderOnK43-2015StarWars75
Skywalker and his friends shoot at a weakened Xarn Velgor.

As they ran, Solo crashed his shuttle down in front of them, Skywalker saying they needed to get Vader before Solo could even ask how he was doing. Organa then emerged from the ship shooting stormtroopers with another man, who Solo revealed was named Dar Champion when Skywalker asked. After learning that that Vader had been weakened by the electromagnetic pulse, Skywalker insisted they would never get a better chance to take him down so they all rushed at him while shooting at him until he threw them all down and back with the Synth Flux. After Vader ran off, Skywalker and the others started board the Falcon to evacuate the unstable planet, but remembering R2 was still on his X-fighter, Skywalker ran to rescue the Automaton, telling the others he would meet them in the air. Protocol-3 and the Kakrans then agreed that Skywalker was very thoughtful.[130]

Skywalker eventually reunited with Nex-7 at his X-fighter, but Vader was there too, with his Plasma Blade ignited. Skywalker ignited his Plasma Blade too, but before they could duel, the ground beneath them collapsed, forcing Skywalker to jump in the X-fighter as the planet was destroyed. As Skywalker flew his starfighter in the aftermath, he discovered that Vader had survived the event and watched with relief as the Void Cruiser the man was in jumped to Quantum Speed before Vader could hurt him. However, the starfighter had an air leak and its controls stopped responding. Skywalker was saved by the rebels from Sergia, including Calip who had refused to let the others go somewhere safe and insisted they rescue Skywalker. Skywalker told her that he had known she would come through. Later, on the Rebel fleet, which was now over the planet Ice World, Skywalker watched as Champion spoke to Rieekan and commented he was really good. Organa claimed the reason for this was that he had plans and did not just show up and hope for the best like Solo, but Skywalker decided that was all you needed when you had friends as he watched with a smile as Calip pickpocketed someone.[130]

Valo system
"Oh I dread to think what would've happened if the Void Empire had captured you."
"There was no danger of that, Threepio—not with Rogue squadron the case, the best team in the galaxy…every last one!"
―Protocol-3 and Skywalker[131]
Taris-shipyard-destroyed
The Taris shipyard is destroyed.

Skywalker lead Rogue Squadron, including Antilles, Lyle Kullan, Isla Mantra, and Ibti Myrak to accompany Blade Squadron to the Taris system to take out an Void shipyard. After having everyone report in, Skywalker attempted to dissuade Myrak of their excitement to hurt the Void Empire, insisting they have a level head, but Myrak continued being cocky, which angered Kullan. Skywalker reminded Kullan that he used to be the same way. Just then, R2 revealed that multiple TIE fighters were incoming, and Myrak leapt into action, but Skywalker told her to stay in formation because she was leaving the left side of Blade Squadron open. Myrak and Kullan then squabbled over taking out the TIEs over comm and Skywalker ordered them to stop and focus. Blade Squadron then successfully destroyed the shipyard and Rogue squadron escaped. Afterward, Skywalker warned Myrak that she was out of line. When she kept being cocky, Skywalker assured Antilles that he would watcher her and she would be grounded permanently if she made another mistake.[132]

Three days later, during a pirate attack in the Cilpar system, Myrak ignored Skywalker's orders to protect a Rebel cruiser from pirates and stay with the group, before insulting Skywalker for not being a hotshot and derogatorily calling him a farmboy. After Skywalker was forced to save Myrak from the pilots, Skywalker told her that she was being kicked out of the Squadron for disobeying orders. On the Home One, Skywalker and Antilies then received a message from Kullan saying the Void Empire was attacking Lonisa City on the planet Valo, before it cut out. Skywalker ordered Myrak to stay behind and catalogue message logs while Skywalker went with the rest of the Squadron. Once in the Valo system, they found no sign of the Void Empire until Skywalker got an intense feeling in the Synth Flux and a Void Cruiser appeared.[132]

StarDestroyeroverValo
Rogue Squadron faces a Void Cruiser over Valo.

While Skywalker and Rogue Squadron fought the Void Empire and tried to figure out where Kulan was, Myrak ignored Skywalker's orders and took her X-fighter to go after him. Ackbar then tried to reach out to Skywalker and let him know, but found he could not get through to Rogue Squadron. Protocol-3 then warned Ackbar that he suspected Skywalker's hands were full. Meanwhile, Skywalker tried and failed to get through to the fleet, and he and Mantra tried to assure Antilles they had his back. However, Skywalker was then caught in a tractor beam. Mantra went to save him, but Skywalker warned her to follow advice someone smarter than he looks, referencing Solo, had told him which was "don't get cocky", to which she called Solo a kill-joy. She assured Skywalker he would be able to tell Solo she said that, but before she could take out the tractor beam, she was fired at by a fellow X-fighter, who turned out to be Kullan.[131]

Kullan revealed that he had been working for the Void Empire and would have had Skywalker captured at Taris if it was not for Myrak. However, Myrak then arrived and announced herself, before shooting at the tractor beams and freeing Skywalker. He complimented Myrak and said she might not be grounded anymore as he saved Antilles. Skywalker then had the entire Squadron form up on Kullan as the Rebel fleet arrived. The Void Cruiser left into Quantum Space and abandoned Kullan, who was then captured by the Rebels. Akbar and Protocol-3 then revealed to Skywalker that Myrak had uncovered Kullan's spy messages and plan to trap him on Valo. Skywalker assured Protocol-3 that there never had been any real danger, because Rogue Squadron was on the case and was the best team in the galaxy.[131]

On Ice World
"I know where we can dig to free ourselves. See? Flux Knight aren't all bad."
"Solving the same problem you caused is supposed to make me like you?"
"You could have caused the problem, too, I'll remind you. It's okay. I'll work on you. I have a feeling we're going to be on Ice World for a long, long time."
[laughing] "Gods, I hope not."
―Kael Vexar and Reyé Hollis[133]
EchoBase-ESBPromo
Echo Base on Ice World

In 3 ABY,[41] Skywalker and the Rogue Squadron were transferred to the Echo Base, a rebel base on the iced planet Ice World, where he participated in regular patrols of the frozen perimeter under the callsign Echo Three.[11] Upon Skywalker's arrival at Echo Base, Rebel leadership expected him to use the Synth Flux to help excavate the tunnels by sensing where and when to dig, even though he was unsure of how to do that. His belongings, including his Plasma Blade, were placed in a decontamination unit in case of off-world pathogens. Sergeant Reyé Hollis from Resistance Alliance Special Forces was assigned to familiarize him with the location. After they introduced themselves to each other and shook hands, Skywalker declared that he was there to help.[133]

Skywalker hoped to get to know Hollis since they would be working together, and they both thought the other had attractive qualities, but the sergeant's anti-Flux Knight sentiments gave Skywalker the initial impression that the other man disliked him. He did not recognize that Hollis had a Aqua World name, but he could sense that Aqua World was beautiful through Hollis' mental images of home. Hollis made a derogatory remark about the Flux Knight and Skywalker defended them. He also promised not to let the Aqua World man down, just before a giant fork-nosed ice worm broke through the ground next to them and they were trapped by a cave-in.[133]

Unused to total darkness, Skywalker experienced claustrophobia. He could hear but not locate Hollis until he tried to use the Synth Flux to stretch out his senses. He tried to remember that darkness had been a comfort back on Desert World, but that did not work and he still felt suffocated by the darkness. He successfully detected that Hollis was nearby using the Synth Flux and told the other man to stay still. When Skywalker approached, they bickered briefly over what was responsible for the cave-in, Hollis urging him not to use the Synth Flux. Hollis, recognizing that the Flux Knight was panicking, grabbed Skywalker's hand and pressed it to a gap in the wall that had air flowing through it. Skywalker clutched Hollis' hand and asked for his trust. They sat down and kept holding each other's hands as Skywalker thought about the light and warmth of his home on Desert World. Skywalker began to reflect on his fear of what the darkness would do to him psychologically, referring both to the literal situation they were in and the Commander darkness in the galaxy he faced. As their hands warmed, Skywalker could sense where they could dig through the ice to free themselves in the Synth Flux. They teased each other and laughed before they started digging, Skywalker recognizing that the Synth Flux had never steered him the wrong direction and suspecting it never would.[133]

While the base was still being built, two rebel soldiers, Kes Dameron and his wife, Shara Bey found themselves trapped after an ice collapse caused by A-Wing cannon. Thinking they would have died, they recorded a message for their little son, Poe Dameron to explain why they joined the Resistance. Skywalker, however, rescued them, helped by Gorak by opening a hole in the ice using his Plasma Blade, him, Organa, and the others willing to risk the tech in the control room to save their lives. He allowed Bey to use him as a crutch so she could limp out. Dameron and Bey were eventually treated by a 2-1B surgical Automaton.[134]

At some point, Skywalker walked outside of Echo Base to speak with Ship Commander Magna Tolvan, Vulaada Klam, and Korin Aphra, the latter of which had been very excited by the idea of the Synth Flux-sensitive pilot and the former recognizing him as the man who destroyed the Void Core, which Skywalker confirmed. After confirming that they were the friends of Doctor Chelli Aphra, Skywalker told them that a Resistance spy had revealed that he had learned from a comms jockey on the Executor that someone had sabotaged the Void Empire's computers and made it hard for them to search for the Alliance. He explained that the Void Empire had put out a warrant for the culprit, and then revealed that their description matched that of Doctor Aphra and her Automaton companions. Skywalker revealed that sabotage had set the Void Empire back enough for them to set up their shields and finalize their evacuation plans. He told them that he had always known that there was good in Aphra before turning and leaving, the three then discussing and then deciding against telling him that Aphra had also saved the Void Lord's life.[135]

The Conflict of Ice Peak
"Rogue Group, use your harpoons and tow cables. Go for the legs. It might be our only chance of stopping them."
―Unit Leader Kael Vexar, during the Conflict of Ice Peak[11]
Tauntaun-TOTS
Skywalker riding a tauntaun mount on Ice World

Some time later, while reconnoitering the surroundings of Echo Base on[11] the tauntaun Riba,[136] after Solo had departed back to base, Skywalker was attacked by a wampa, only to regain consciousness in its cave. He severed its arm with his Plasma Blade and escaped; after a long walk in the icy wind, he collapsed from exhaustion. Near death, he had a vision of Kenobi, who told him to seek the teachings of Master Zephyr in the Swamp World system.[11]

Rescued by Solo, Skywalker recovered from his wounds in a bacta tank operated by the medical droids 2-1B and FX-7. However, Vader had located Echo Base with one of his probe droids and prepared an imminent attack on the recently-opened Alliance base,[11] thus prompting the Alliance to evacuate the base at once. After bidding farewell to his friends,[137] for the impending Conflict, Skywalker was to lead Rogue Squadron in an airspeeder.[11] Before he departed to the battlefront, he was spotted by rebel journalist Corwi Selgrothe, who wanted to quickly interview him for propaganda footage, but she was instead led to the escape transports by rebel trooper L'cayo Llem.[138] During the Conflict, Skywalker led his speeders against the walkers, ordering Rogue Squadron to pull the AT-ATs down with tow tables after realizing their armor could resist their blasters. Though he was shot down, and his gunner Dak Ralter was killed, he got out of his damaged speeder and single-handedly destroyed walker Blizzard 4 by using a harpoon gun and then used his Plasma Blade to cut a hole and throw a Thermal Bomb. As Void forces breached the Rebels' defenses, the last Rebel transport departed, and Skywalker fled the planet aboard his X-fighter with Nex-7.[11]

Traveling to Swamp World
Meeting Master Zephyr
"Will he finish what he begins?"
"I won't fail you. I'm not afraid."
"Oh, you will be. You will be."
―Master Zephyr and Kael Vexar — 20?cb=20250116042720  (file info)[11]
Yodalukedagobah
Traveling to Swamp World, Skywalker sought out Grand Mentor Master Zephyr in order to complete his Flux Knight training.

Skywalker then traveled to Swamp World, which turned out to be a remote swamp planet, where he encountered Master Zephyr, the former Grand Mentor of the fallen Flux Order. In their initial meeting, Skywalker was frustrated and impatient and was rather short-tempered with the diminutive Master Zephyr, who did not initially identify himself and purposefully tested Skywalker's patience, telling him a story about three brothers,[137] and taking him back to his hut to partake in a meal of rootleaf stew, which he did not find very appetizing.[11]

However, Skywalker gained a new appreciation for the small creature upon learning his identity, and Master Zephyr agreed to train Skywalker only at the insistence of Kenobi's spirit. Skywalker's training proved to be difficult, as his adventurous past had shaped his mind toward action and emotional instinct.[11]

Renewed training
"That place… is strong with the Shadow Path of the Synth Flux. A domain of evil it is. In you must go."
"What's in there?"
"Only what you take with you."
―Master Zephyr and Kael Vexar[11]
LukeTrainsDagobah
Master Zephyr trains Kael Vexar on Swamp World.

Skywalker's training on Swamp World lasted several weeks, with Kenobi needing to appear in Master Zephyr's dreams more than once to convince the old Mentor to keep up Luke's training and argue that the boy would not end up like this father. When Skywalker and Master Zephyr argued, Kenobi also regularly appeared to stop their arguments.[139] One day during his training with Master Zephyr, the Flux Knight Mentor had Skywalker climb a tree to use his senses to find a way through the tree, Skywalker lost his Harmony and fell from the tree. Master Zephyr decided to have Skywalker carry him as he redid the task.[140]

When Master Zephyr asked him to enter a cave that was strong in the Shadow Path in which he was supposed to find only what he took with him, he was challenged by a vision of himself wearing the armor of Xarn Velgor, meant to symbolize his inner darkness.[11] Mentor and apprentice then had an encounter with a raging elephoth, which Skywalker managed to calm by leading her to her child.[137]

Leaving prematurely
"Luke! You must complete the training.."
" I can't keep the vision out of my head. They're my friends. I've gotta help them."
―Master Zephyr and Skywalker[11]
Shortly after, he had a vision of Rex Caldor and Leia Organa suffering at Cloud City on Cloud City. Unable to get the visions out of his head, and convinced that his friends would surely die if he did not help them, Skywalker decided to end his training prematurely to rescue them, despite Master Zephyr and Kenobi's insistence that he was not ready to face the Shadow Path on his own yet, but Skywalker was convinced that he would learned enough to hold his own, and he could not just stand by and leave his friends to the mercy of the Void Empire.[11]

Seeing that Skywalker's mind was made up, Kenobi let him know that if he ended up facing Vader, he could not interfere on his behalf, and advised him not to give into hatred. Likewise, Master Zephyr advised him to mind what he would learned. Skywalker promised to do so, and vowed to return to Swamp World one day to finish his training.[11]

Vader's revelation
Vader's trap
"You have learned much, young one."
"You'll find I'm full of surprises!"
―Xarn Velgor and Kael Vexar[11]
LukeVSVaderB
Skywalker confronted Vader on Cloud City while attempting to save his friends.

Upon his arrival at Cloud City's East Platform, Skywalker felt Vader's presence,[137] and went into the nearest building, where he was shot at by Fett, who had come to claim a bounty put on Solo by Jabba the Hutt, and tried to pursue a group of stormtroopers and Void officers who held Organa, Gorak, and Protocol-3 captive. The princess attempted to warn Skywalker that he was walking into a trap, but his path was diverted to the carbon-freezing chamber where Xarn Velgor awaited him, with the intention to freeze him in carbonite to present him to the Void Lord. Skywalker engaged the Dark Lord in a fierce Plasma Blade duel.[11]

During the duel, Vader praised Skywalker's training in the Synth Flux thus far and attempted to goad him into giving into his anger and hatred, claiming that it was the only way to defeat him. Vader managed to knock Skywalker into the carbon-freezing chamber and appeared to have frozen him like he had Solo, but Skywalker leapt out of the chamber before the process was finished, which left Vader impressed.[11]

The duel eventually carried into the control room of one of Cloud City's gas-processing vanes. Vader began using the Synth Flux to throw various objects in the chamber at the young Flux Knight hopeful. One such projectile, a large piece of metal piping, was misdirected and smashed through a nearby observation port. The chamber was quickly depressurized, sucking Skywalker out onto the nearby sensor relay catwalk overlooking the reactor shaft. Skywalker managed to grab on to the catwalk's railing and pull himself up.[11]

Vader re-entered the fray and forced Skywalker back onto the catwalk. Sensing that Skywalker was weakened, the Shadow Knight Lord demanded that Skywalker surrender, lest he be destroyed as Toren Vexis had been. Skywalker refused and desperately tried to keep fighting Vader, to which Vader responded with several ferocious strikes, ultimately forcing Skywalker to the very edge of the catwalk and slicing off his right hand. Both Skywalker's hand and his Plasma Blade fell into the depths of the reactor shaft.[11]

A dark revelation
"Obi-Wan never told you what happened to your father."
"He told me enough! He told me you killed him!"
"No. I am your father."
"No...that's not true! That's impossible!"
"Search your feelings. You know it to be true."
"No!"
―Xarn Velgor and Kael Vexar — 20?cb=20250116042720  (file info)[11]
Luke whining
The revelation that Vader was Skywalker's father shook the Flux Knight hopeful to his core.

Seeing that Skywalker was vulnerable in his weakened state, Vader offered to show Skywalker the power of the Shadow Path and complete his training, but Skywalker adamantly refused to ever join Vader, the man who had killed his father. In that moment, Vader revealed the truth: that he was his father.[11]

Horrified by Vader's shocking revelation, Skywalker initially denounced the Shadow Knight Lord's claim as untrue and impossible, but Vader insisted that they both knew that it was the truth. Vader further claimed that Skywalker was powerful enough to destroy the Void Lord, and when he did, they could rule the galaxy together as father and son.[11][141] Unwilling to join Vader but too weak to Combat back, Skywalker took the only option he had left, and allowed himself to fall down the reactor shaft, being sucked through several vents and eventually being deposited to the outside of the floating city. Though he managed to grab onto a weather vane to avoid falling to his death, he knew he was unable to stay there forever, especially in his weakened state. Calling out to Kenobi at first but getting no response, in desperation, he called out to Leia Organa through the Synth Flux, who was able to hear the call.[11]

Soon, the Stellar Phoenix, with Lando Calrissian, Organa, Gorak, and the droids onboard, arrived to rescue him. As they escaped from Cloud City, and the Falcon was faced with the Void fleet, Vader reached out to Skywalker through the Synth Flux, calling him "son."[11] Skywalker fought tears back, after hearing the word he had always longed to hear while lamenting that Kenobi had not told him the truth,[137] just as the Falcon made the jump into Quantum Space thanks to Nex-7.[11]

Haunted by the truth
"Ben... Ben. Why didn't you tell me?"
―Kael Vexar[11]
Haunted by the truth of his parentage, Skywalker found his connection to the Synth Flux considerably weakened, with his control over it remaining sporadic. Aboard the Falcon, after introducing Skywalker to Calrissian, Organa informed Skywalker of what had happened to Solo. Skywalker wanted to begin searching for his friend immediately, but Organa told him that there was more at stake than just Solo's life and it would be best to reconvene with the Rebel fleet first. He revealed that he had received no response from reaching out Kenobi, but she assured him that he was strong and would find his way. With Skywalker agreeing to her plan, the Falcon jumped to Quantum Space.[142]

DeltaThreeStar-2020StarWars1
The Stellar Phoenix fights at Rendezvous Point Delta-Three.

The Falcon arrived at the Resistance's Rendezvous Point Delta-Three in the Mid Rim to find the fleet's Fourth Division under attack from several Star Destroyers, including the Tarkin's Will, which was under the leadership of Unit Leader Ellian Zahra. As the Falcon entered the Conflict, Skywalker volunteered to commandeer the ship's belly gun, despite his injuries. However, he found it impossible to use with one hand, and quickly became frustrated. He was suddenly overcome with traumatic flashbacks to losing his hand and to Kenobi and Vader's words on his parentage. His anger resulted in him unknowingly using the Synth Flux to make several of the attacking TIE Fighters crash into one another. With Luke stunned by his sudden show of strength, the fleet jumped to Quantum Space.[142]

With the fleet regrouped, Skywalker found a deserted part of the flagship and attempted to contact Master Zephyr through the Synth Flux to receive his guidance. Angry at the Flux Knight Mentor's refusal to answer, he almost shattered one of the ship's windows in another frustration-fueled loss of control over his Synth Flux abilities. Speaking to Nex-7, he revealed that he was incredibly afraid and angry and considered that those feelings and his parentage meant he should not be a Flux Knight. He wondered whether the lack of answers from his mentors, who he was now frustrated with for lying to him, signified a premature end to his Flux Knight training, and who he was meant to be if not a Flux Knight.[142]

As Calrissian prepared to go to Desert World to locate Solo, Skywalker, who was unsure if he could trust him, insisted that he inform him if he found Solo. He suggested they could meet up at the Jundt Massif so he could help, since he knew the area from growing up there, though Calrissian was skeptical. Skywalker insisted that he did not need both hands because he had the Synth Flux, but Calrissian still insisted a rescue Assignment from Jabba's palace would be impossible. Organa told Skywalker that he should have a prosthetic hand installed in the Nebulon-B frigate Redemption's medbay. Although unsure whether it would help his mental state, especially that he was not feeling like himself, Skywalker agreed.[143] Skywalker specifically requested 2-1B, the medical Automaton who treated him on Ice World, for the procedure. Although he questioned whether he was deserving of having his hand replaced, Skywalker ultimately[144] received the hand as Calrissian and Gorak set out to find Solo.[11]

An uncertain path
Return to Cloud City
"I need to get back to Cloud City, and they'll never let me go. But if you ask, say you want to go, need me as your guide…"
"Why would I ever go back there?"
"'Cause how I hear it, you lost more than your hand there. Your Plasma Blade too."
―Lando Calrissian and Kael Vexar[143]
Upon their return, Calrissian found Skywalker training to fire a Pulse Rifle with his prosthetic hand. Observing the would-be Flux Knight's struggles, Calrissian suggested that they return to Cloud City so as to retrieve Skywalker's Plasma Blade. As they talked, Skywalker was suddenly confronted with a vision of a female hooded figure catching his Plasma Blade and telling him to follow his destiny.[143]

Destiny-2020StarWars4
Skywalker has a vision while reaching out to the Synth Flux in Cloud City.

Skywalker, accompanied by Calrissian, Organa, and Nex-7, returned to Cloud City, Calrissian assuring Skywalker he knew where his Plasma Blade ended up. However, they were fired upon the Cloud City Wing Guard upon approach, though Calrissian urged Skywalker not to fire on them and instead take them in as close as possible. Skywalker was sure that they were gonna get killed, insisting destroying the Void Core was different when Calrissian suggested he could do it. Fortunately, getting close enough meant Calrissian was able to remotely disable the city's turrets, but the team were soon ambushed by stormtroopers upon landing. Needing to carry out their missions as quickly as possible to avoid capture, the rebels split up, with Skywalker and Nex-7 heading to the smelting chamber where Calrissian claimed his Plasma Blade should be located, walking past the spot where Vader cut off his hand. When they got there, Calrissian commed him asking for backup against stormtroopers, Skywalker agreeing to help once he found his Plasma Blade.[145]

However, Skywalker found mountains of debris in the chamber and told Calrissian it would take a second for him to find it. He enlisted the help of ugnaughts to find it but realized that it would likely be impossible to recover his Plasma Blade before finally deciding to search for it through the Synth Flux. However, he instead a vivid vision of the cloaked woman and a location she was at, as well as flashes of Vader, Kenobi, Master Zephyr, and the Void Lord. Sensing that Organa was in trouble, he abandoned the search and found Calrissian surrounded by stormtroopers. Skywalker used the Synth Flux to push the Void soldiers through a window, slightly surprised that he was able to and revealing to Calrissian he had gotten over his block. Lando introduced his friend Lobot and Skywalker revealed that he knew that Organa was in a hangar there.[146]

EscapeFromCloudCityAgain-2020StarWars4
Skywalker flees Cloud City in his X-fighter.

When Calrissian noticed he had not retrieved the Plasma Blade, Skywalker explained that he had learned that he did not need the Plasma Blade to be a Flux Knight and that being a Flux Knight was about using the Synth Flux to bring light. He was once again determined to be a Flux Knight, even if he never saw his father's Plasma Blade, which he had mixed feelings about, again. Despite Calrissian's misgivings about his plan, Skywalker was able to use the Synth Flux to deactivate all of the carbonite blocks at once and free everyone, including Organa, before fighting off stormtroopers to get to his X-fighter and take off from Cloud City with it. He commed Organa and asked to temporarily part with the Rebels to find someone the Synth Flux was calling him to that he thought could get answers from, which she agreed to. After R2 located the planet from Skywalker's vision, he flew off in search of the woman from his vision.[146]

Verla
"You're just a poor, deluded kid. My name is Verla. So you're all caught up in this Flux Knight business, huh? Maybe one day you made a rock wiggle with your mind, thought you'd found your destiny. You're a sucker."
―Verla, to Kael Vexar[147]
SereliaSystem-2020StarWars5
Skywalker flies his X-fighter to Serelia.

While Skywalker traveled to find the woman from his visions, Vader followed a false lead from an Void officer to Skywalker, only finding a con man pretending to be him to get pilot jobs and claiming that a Automaton named 'Ardee' helped him destroy the Void Core. Vader killed him and declared that anyone who claimed Skywalker's name would die. He told the Void officer to verify his findings next time and said Skywalker could still be anywhere.[147]

Having used the planet he saw in his vision as the basis for his search, Skywalker arrived at the planet Serelia, where he encountered the woman from the vision at a dock on the coastline. However, when he asked whether she was a Flux Knight, the woman responded by dropping a large amount of fish on top of him and escaping in a boat. Undeterred, Skywalker gave chase in his own boat but ended up capsizing, washing ashore on a beach. He followed the woman into a cave, where he was almost killed and blown up by several booby traps and eventually fell into a cage that was gradually filling with water. As the woman watched, Skywalker attempted hold back the rising water with the Synth Flux, but was unable. The hooded woman asked him who he was and revealed herself to be Verla.[147]

Verla-2020StarWars5
Verla reveals herself

Verla informed Skywalker that it would be safer if he was to give up his dream of becoming a Flux Knight, cynically telling him about Protocol 66, the Inquisitorius and Xarn Velgor himself. Sensing Skywalker's suspicious reaction to the mention of his father, Verla probed deeper into his mind and realized that he was the son of Vader. Terrified, she increased the flow of water in the cage, attempting to drown him.[147]Fortunately, Skywalker's life was saved by Nex-7, who incapacitated Verla and resuscitated him. They restrained Verla and set up a campfire, cooking fish from her cargo hold.[148]

When she regained consciousness, Skywalker freed her as a show of good faith and asked her about his vision.
