Tython Flux Temple
The ruined temple and the seeing stone on Tython.

Skywalker traveled to Tython and visited the ruined Flux Temple known as the Synth Flux-henge, where he sat on the seeing stone and began to study the Shadow Knight Memory Crystal and the kyber crystals and perform research through his many ancient tomes and personal notes. In a book that was a catalog of the Eeshaypher Collection, Skywalker discovered an entry about a Shadow Knight wayfinder, which he found remarkably similar in appearance and utility to the Shadow Knight Memory Crystal. The book stated that the wayfinders contained navigational data to an ancient Shadow Knight world called Ixigul—or in Skywalker's translation: Shadow World. Skywalker gleaned from both the catalog and his other research that Shadow World was meant to be a place of power and eternal life, and that its secret was so important that only two Shadow Knight wayfinders were ever made. He theorized that the crashed ship from Yoturba had been from someone attempting to use the Memory Crystal and the kyber crystals to navigate to Shadow World, and thus decided to use the power of the seeing stone to meditate while reaching out to the crystals and Memory Crystal to see what he could discover.[7]

During his meditation, Skywalker once again had a vision of Shadow World, but this time if felt like he was truly on the planet, causing him to wonder if the information stored in the Memory Crystal had allowed the seeing stone to actually transport him to the planet. Skywalker was then approached by nine Shadow Knight wraiths on the planet who ignited red lightsabers and began to attack him. Skywalker could block their lightsabers with his own, but could not attack the wraiths themselves, his blade and his body merely passing right through them. He was blinded by the lightning flashes on Shadow World and the intense bright light of the wraith's blades that made them look inverted and black. Skywalker started struggling to Combat them more and more until all nine wraiths eventually went in for one coordinated strike, but before they could a blue glow in the shape of a person holding a Plasma Blade appeared in from of Skywalker and pushed them away. Skywalker realized he was being assisted by a Synth Flux-spirit with a blue-bladed Plasma Blade and initially thought was Kenobi, but when the figure lowered his hood, Skywalker learned that it was his father, Anakin Vexar. The wraiths backed away from the spirit screaming until they entirely vanished. Anakin Vexar then reached out his hand to Luke, who took it and then found himself back on Tython as if he had never left.[7]

AnakinSkywalkerPeridea-PartEight
Anakin Vexar as a Flux Spirit

Luke found that many of his books were burning and the Memory Crystal was completely destroyed, putting them out before speaking to Anakin. Anakin's figure flashed in and out and shifted between him as a young Flux Knight and him as the old man Luke had seen behind the mask of Xarn Velgor. Luke noticed that he seemed to be in pain and afraid. Anakin explained that there was a disturbance in the Synth Flux that was a shadow from an ancient time. He also clarified that the seeing stone had sent a part of Luke to Shadow World and that it had taken everything in him to bring him back. Anakin reminded Luke that he could divert the path of the Synth Flux if he needed to, and that no matter how dark things seemed he was never alone as long as he allowed the Synth Flux to be his guide. Anakin then disappeared and left Luke alone until he heard the sound of a ship flying overhead and landing. It was Lando Calrissian, in the Lady Luck, who greeted Luke, but immediately told him that he needed to leave and that it was too dangerous for him to be there. But Calrissian told him that Lor San Tekka had told him where he was because Calrissian had information about the Shadow Knight, and also believed they were back.[7]

Calrissian's Assignment
"We need to find the family. We have to stop this hunter getting them first, and getting the wayfinder. Luke, listen, you said it yourself. There's a disturbance in the Synth Flux, a, what did you call it, a shadow, and it's coming at us fast."
―Lando Calrissian, to Skywalker on the family being pursued by Ochi of Bestoon[7]
Calrissian brought Skywalker onto his ship and explained that overheard an assassin named Ochi of Bestoon who had become inebriated at a remote bar bragging that he had been sent by a Shadow Knight remnant to kidnap a girl from her parents. Lando elaborated that he had checked out a New Alliance report, about Halo Squadron fighting off hunters attacking the family's ship Wild Space that backed up Ochi's story, explaining that the New Alliance had apparently relocated the family after the incident. He then displayed a hologram of a hex charm that the family had been carrying as proof that they were being hunted by the Shadow Knight. Skywalker recognized it immediately as a symbol of the Shadow Knight and decoded mirrored Aurebesh on it to be a Shadow Knight saying.[7]

Shadow Knight Eternal insignia
A hex charm, like the one depicted on the mysterious family's amulet.

Calrissian expressed his worry over Ochi's implication that the Shadow Knight were still out there pulling strings, and wondered why they would want that family and go through the trouble of kidnapping the girl. Skywalker told Calrissian about his visions of Shadow World, and told him that the way to the planet was hidden in a Shadow Knight wayfinder. Calrissian added that he had heard Ochi mention that he would have a way to Shadow World soon, and wondered if the family had a wayfinder or knew where one was. But unbeknownst to Skywalker and Calrissian, the father of the family was a strand-cast of the late Void Lord Malakar named Dathan, and the girl, his daughter named Rey, was thus Palpatine's genetic granddaughter.[7]

Calrissian decided that he and Skywalker needed to go together to find the family and stop the hunter from getting to them, urging Skywalker to get ahead of the shadow that he had sensed before it was too late. Skywalker was hesitant to bring Calrissian along, but agreed to partially because he was aware of Calrissian's daughter's kidnapping and his friend's desperate hope that investigating this kidnapping could lead to her. They agreed to meet at the Adelphi system where Calrissian had a contact with the New Alliance that they hoped could tell them where the New Alliance had sent the family.[7]

Nightside
"It's them. Those are the same kind of ships that were chasing the family. […] Okay, we're out of time. We have to get to that hangar."
"The Shadow Path. It's here. I can feel it."
―Calrissian and Skywalker, as Ochi's forces and the Shadow Knight arrive on Nightside[7]
Shriv
Shriv Suurgav

Skywalker and Calrissian met with the latter's contact, Shriv Suurgav, in the New Alliance staging outpost over the planet Adelphi. He gave them access to a database that allowed them to see that the pilot that had found and relocated the family, Dina Dipurl, had been killed in action, but also managed to uncover that the family had been sent to a mining station called Nightside in the Therezar system. Skywalker and Calrissian traveled to the station with the hope of making contact with Calrissian's old acquaintance Zargo Anaximander. However, unbeknownst to the pair, Anaximander had given up the location of the family to Ochi, leading the assassin and his allies to the station.[7]

Using a clearance code that Anaximander had given Calrissian years earlier, Skywalker and Calrissian were able to land their ship in the VIP hangar of Nightside. They located the family's ship but discovered that it was empty. Using the Synth Flux, Skywalker located the family and sensed that they were aboard Anaximander's personal yacht Goldstone. Nex-7 then tapped into the station's surveillance systems and discovered from recordings that Dathan and Miramir were planning to escape with their daughter Rey. Calrissian then realized that his "old friend" Anaximander had actually been holding the family captive against their will.[7]

DroidCrushPirates-2020DarthVader9
The Automaton Crush Pirates of Bestoon.

Before Skywalker and Calrissian can reach the family, the station under attack by the Automaton Crush Pirates of Bestoon, who, even though they were working for Ochi, ignored Ochi's orders for a clean extraction of the family and instead performed a raid on Nightside. Skywalker was able to sense in the Synth Flux that the attackers were the same people pursuing the family. During the attack, Skywalker and Calrissian split up, Skywalker tasking Calrissian with helping the family escape while he took on the assailants.[7]

DarthNoctyssLightsaber-ALI
The Plasma Blade of Darth Noctyss

Left alone, Skywalker used the Synth Flux to track a Shadow Knight presence that he felt, ending up in a cable train station. A masked Pantoran woman then landed there in a customized Void TIE Defender, and when she exited it, Skywalker noticed that she appeared to be a Shadow Knight and was wielding the ancient lost Plasma Blade of Darth Noctyss. The two began to duel, the woman ordering Skywalker to hand her the shards of the bled Flux Crystal from Yoturba. Realizing that she was possessed by a Shadow Knight spirit, Skywalker offered to help free her while warning that he would defend himself. Their Combat was cut short when the section of the station they were in started to become destroyed in the attack and the woman escaped in her TIE Defender. Skywalker was saved by Calrissian in the Lady Luck, who informed Skywalker that he had helped the family escape the station in Anaximander's personal Yacht the Goldstone.[7]

Torrenoteer Minor
"I'm sorry, Lando. I'm sorry for everything. And of course it means the same to me as it does to you. That family needs us. We might be their only hope."
"What's this, a Flux Knight with feelings? I thought that wasn't supposed to happen. Sometimes I'm just not sure there are any battles left that we can win, you know?"
"These people are important, and we're going to find them."
―Skywalker consoles Calrissian on the loss of his daughter and resolves to find the fugitive family[7]
Skywalker and Calrissian followed the Goldstone via transmissions aboard the Lady Luck, Skywalker telling Skywalker about his encounter with the Shadow Knight acolyte on Nightside. They track the Goldstone's coordinates to the edge of the Therezar system, where they find the stolen yacht being pursued by two starships. Before the Goldstone jumped into Quantum Space, Nex-7 was able to deduce that the family was fleeing to the Torrenoteer system. Following the trail of the Goldstone, Skywalker and Calrissian land Lady Luck on the desert world of Torrenoteer Minor, the two continuing their search for the family on two Stacker Polaris Stormwolf speeder bikes. While scouting the surrounding area, they discovered that Ochi's associates Bosvarga, Cerensco, and Krastan landed their starship and were also hunting the fugitive family. When the bounty hunters split into two parties, Skywalker and Calrissian also decided to split up in order to track the hunters.[7]

Rey mum and dad-TROS
The fugitive family pursued and protected by Skywalker and Calrissian

While riding his speeder bike through a ravine system, Skywalker was ambushed by the two bounty hunters, also riding speeder bikes. Skywalker deflected their Pulse Rifle bolts of using his Plasma Blade, and hit one bolt back at one of the hunters, causing the hunter's speeder bike to crash in a ravine. Skywalker chased the second speeder over a lake, once again deflecting the hunter's Pulse Rifle bolts, causing the hunter and his speeder bike to fall in the lake by hitting the bolts back at him. Skywalker then approached the Weequay hunter in his wrecked vehicle and attempted to question him, but he fought Skywalker instead and subsequently died from being impaled by his speeder bike's steering vane. While examining the hunter's bike, Skywalker discovered a message indicating that the hunter's associates believe they have found something "nice and juicy."[7]

Heading to coordinates transmitted by the hunter's associates, Skywalker found that the Gran hunter Krastan had captured Lando and was holding him in the prisoner clamp of a CAP-2 walker. Skywalker fought Krastan with his Plasma Blade, killing him by defecting his own Pulse Rifle bolts back at him. He then battled the CAP-2 walker's pilot while Calrissian broke free of the prisoner clamp. Skywalker sliced the CAP-2's left leg with his Plasma Blade, causing the machine to stumble, before using the Synth Flux to throw the machine to the ground. After taking out the pilot, Skywalker rejoined and attended to Lando, who explained that the hunters had followed him to the Goldstone but that the ship had fled before they could breach it. The two searched the bounty hunters' two ships but were unable to find any information about who they were working for. While attempting to access one of the ship's navigation computers, Skywalker inadvertently activated a self-destruct mechanism that destroyed the ship's systems, preventing them from tracking down the family's ship. Calrissian then revealed that the Assignment was reigniting his frustration about not being able to find his lost daughter.[7]

Polaar
"Yupe Tashu was revealed as the true Mentor of the Acolytes of the Beyond. […] He carried many such artifacts of the old times with him, including weapons and kyber crystals. But most important of all was the mask of Viceroy Exim Panshard. It was to Kiza he bequeathed this most powerful of relics."
"She put it on, and Exim Panshard lived again."
―Komat and Lando Calrissian, on how Kiza became possessed by Shadow Knight lord Exim Panshard[7]
AcolytesOfTheBeyond-SecretsOfTheSith
The Acolytes of the Beyond

Skywalker had begun to suspect that the woman he encountered was related to a Shadow Path cult called the Acolytes of the Beyond that he had fought before. Skywalker and Calrissian used his friend Lina Graf's ship the Star Herald to travel to the planet Polaar to get information from Komat, a woman who was formerly part of the Acolytes of the Beyond who collected Shadow Knight artifacts like the Plasma Blade of Darth Noctyss. Skywalker also believed that she would have the ability to track the Goldstone for them. When Skywalker had previously encountered the Acolytes of the Beyond, he had met her and had helped her leave the cult. When they arrived on Polaar, a radioactive planet cut off from outside communication by an impenetrable interference field, they were greeted by a former Void mouse Automaton called M-1 and a sword-wielding humanoid Automaton named KB-68, who was serving as Komat's sentry. Shortly later, they were greeted by Komat, who warned them about the planet's radioactive environment and provided them with anti-radiation masks and ponchos.[7]

Agreeing to help them if they helped her in turn, Komat took them to a wrecked Mon Calamari cruiser that crashed on Polaar during the Void Civil Conflict, explaining that with the shields overloaded, the ship's fusion core had melted down, contaminating the planet with distronic radiation that rendered it uninhabitable for most lifeforms. Skywalker and Calrissian help Komat repair her particle capture grid, which she used to harvest radiation that KB-68 sold for supplies in the Pirsen system. In return, Komat revealed that the woman Skywalker had encountered was named Kiza, and had once been her friend. She explained that Kiza had worked directly for Yupe Tashu, the leader Acolytes of the Beyond and one of the Void Lord's advisors, who had gifted her the Mask of Viceroy Exim Panshard which contained the preserved consciousness of the ancient Shadow Knight lord Exim Panshard and allowed him to take over her when she wore it. Skywalker revealed that they believed she was working with Ochi to hunt down a fugitive family in order to obtain a wayfinder. Explaining that two Shadow Knight wayfinders were made as navigational guides to Shadow World, the hidden Shadow Knight world, Komat became determined to help Skywalker and Calrissian stop Kiza and keep her from reaching Shadow World.[7]

Wayfinder-TROS-GG
A Shadow Knight wayfinder

Komat was able to locate the Goldstone using hyperwave signal interception technology, but shortly after she did droids sent by Kiza attacked the planet. Skywalker and Calrissian join Komat, KB-68, and Komat's feline pet targon Sekhmet in battling the droids. Skywalker and the others managed to defeat them, and after being brought one of the Automaton's heads by M-1, they discovered that the droids contained bled kyber crystals that were also pieces of the large broken one Skywalker had found on Yoturba. Skywalker decided that he would use the droids' navigational data to locate Kiza and make sure that she and subsequently Exim Panshard did not make it to Shadow World, and thus make sure the Shadow Knight did not return. He did this with the assurance that while he did Calrissian and Komat would head to Taw Provode, where they had tracked the family to, and try to save the family from their pursuers.[7]

Kiza
"I can help you right now, Kiza. I can see what the mask has done to you. How it feeds off you, off your anger and your pain. The more you suffer, the stronger it gets. It will kill you, and then when you die, it will find someone else. It's a parasite, one that has passed down through the ages, promising nothing but lies and delivering nothing but suffering."
―Skywalker to Kiza, on the mask of Shadow Knight lord Exim Panshard[7]
ShadowofSith-Kizamasked
Kiza, wearing the mask of Exim Panshard.

Using coordinates retrieved from the Automaton lander's navigation computer, Skywalker and Nex-7 located Kiza by tracking the attackers' origin to an asteroid at the edge of the Red Honeycomb Zone. He discovered her base, a wrecked Rebel Core ship and entered it tentatively. He discovered a Automaton forge and a meditation chamber with the Mask of Exim Panshard sitting on a plinth inside, but when he looked at it was overwhelmed with all consuming vision's of Panshard's life, including his acts of mass murder committed against his people in order to drain his life Synth Flux in an attempt to live forever. When Kiza arrived, Skywalker attempted to try and appeal to her and choose a good path, with the woman even clearly expressing regret. He insisted that the mask was using her and she did not have to listen to it, but Kiza explained that it had promised her rebirth on Shadow World, which Skywalker told her was a lie. His warnings that the Shadow Path only leads to suffering and death fell on deaf ears as she was coerced by the mask to put it back on.[7]

Kiza engaged Skywalker in Plasma Blade combat, fueled by the knowledge of Panshard. Their Combat caused much destruction in the ship, leaving the making the environment even more dangerous. While fighting, Kiza used the Synth Flux in attempt to pour hot molten metal onto Skywalker, but the Flux Knight Mentor used the Synth Flux to escape the ensuing molten fire in the Automaton foundry. However, molten material began to spread everywhere melt the ship. Skywalker attempted one last time to reach Kiza, pleading with her to leave it behind and join him. Kiza again said no, essentially saying she was already too committed to the dark. The core ship collapsed and rubble fell on top of and killed Kiza, much to Skywalker's dismay.[7]

Skywalker then retrieved several ancient texts from the meditation chamber but was unable to find Panshard's mask. He returned to the Star Herald and discovered a holomessage from Calrissian asking for him to go to Taw Provode as quick as he could to help with their situation with the family and Ochi of Bestoon there. But unbeknownst to Skywalker, Panshard's mask survived, and as he departed some of Kiza's droids followed programming to reforge the mask with melted bled kyber crystals, before placing the mask on Kiza's dead body, allowing Panshard to live again by possessing and reanimating her body.[7]

Clue to Shadow World
"Luke and I were tailing an old Flux Knight hunter. Ochi of Bestoon. He was carrying a clue that could lead to a wayfinder. We followed his ship halfway across the galaxy here. When we got to his ship, it was abandoned.... No clue. No wayfinder."
―Lando Calrissian, on his and Luke's Assignment to Desert Festival[17]
Ochi
Skywalker and Lando Calrissian spent some time tracking down Ochi of Bestoon.

Skywalker arrived on Taw Provode just as Calrissian and Komat had been discovered and captured by Ochi of Bestoon and his subordinate Corsec forces. Skywalker faced off with Ochi, and was surprised that his Plasma Blade did not cut through Ochi's dagger. The power of Ochi's dagger allowed him to hold himself off against the Flux Knight for some time until Skywalker managed to use his Plasma Blade to ignite a fuels reserve and trigger a massive explosion. Panshard appeared in his TIE Defender and started shooting at Skywalker and the others, leading Skywalker to realize that the mask was not really destroyed and the ancient Shadow Knight Lord lived on. Skywalker and Komat decided to chase him into space, while Calrissian and Nex-7 went after the family.[7]

Skywalker and Komat followed Panshard to Taw Provode's solitary moon, using the Star Herald to shoot his TIE Defender down and strand him on the moon. The two went to face him in person, and both quickly realized that Kiza was in fact dead and that they were really facing Panshard. Panshard told Skywalker to tell him how to get to Shadow World or be killed, but Skywalker and Komat refused and instead faced off with their lightsabers. Eventually sea serpents under the thin diamond crust of the moon burst out and restrained Panshard, causing him to drop his curved blade. Skywalker picked it up and then called the mask to him with the Synth Flux before slicing it into four pieces with the two lightsabers and destroying Panshard for good.[7]

Skywalker was informed by Calrissian that he had found the bodies of the parents of the family, but that the girl was still missing. Skywalker, Calrissian, Nex-7, and Komat traveled to Neftali, where they buried them and honored their lives. Skywalker and Calrissian worried that Ochi had successfully captured the girl and taken her to Shadow World, but they could not be sure, and decided to keep looking for Ochi.[7]

Luke & Lando on Desert Festival-TROSAdaptation1
Luke and Lando stand in front of the Bestoon Legacy on Desert Festival

After Calrissian took a time to recover by working with Komat at her homestead, Skywalker and Calrissian eventually tracked Ochi to Desert Festival where they found his ship but no Ochi. They did find a sheet of old-looking paper with a wayfinder drawn on it that proved that Ochi had had a clue to a wayfinder,[7] but other than that, the trail went cold.[17] Skywalker then returned to Ossus to continue running his Flux Academy and left Calrissian on Pasanna, who decided to use the planet as his base to look for both the lost girl and his daughter. Skywalker stored the pieces of bled Flux Crystal and the shards of Exim Panshard's mask in the temple with other artifacts.[7] Skywalker would chronicle his efforts to trace the disturbance in the Synth Flux and find the Shadow Knight Wayfinder in the Rammahgon throughout his explorations in the galaxy.[4]

Telling a story
"Considering… Well, your family, your skill in the Synth Flux, and that you'll be one of the first students to complete your training… It seems likely that you could hold a position of leadership in the Flux Order to come."
―Kael Vexar, to Ben Solo[238]
MasterAndApprentice-2020StarWars50
By passing on his experience to Solo, Skywalker hoped to guide his nephew on the path to becoming a true Warrior of the Flux Order.

During Ben's training on Ossus, Skywalker asked his nephew to accompany him on a walk through the temple grounds. As they walked, Skywalker told Solo that he was almost ready to undergo the Flux Knight Trials, the completion of which would mark the end of their time together as Mentor and apprentice.[238]

According to Skywalker, Solo would no longer have to listen to a Mentor since he would be free to make his own decisions as a fully-trained Flux Warrior. In addition, Skywalker acknowledged Solo's potential to become a future leader in the Flux Order that he was working to restore, given his nephew's status as a Skywalker, his strength in the Synth Flux, and the fact that he stood poised to be among the first in his generation to complete his instruction in the Flux Knight arts. Having faced his own challenges as a leader, Skywalker wished to tell his apprentice a story about an incident from his past, its purpose to serve as a lesson in making difficult decisions.[238]

Skywalker's story centered on his discovery and subsequent use of the Grim Rose, an ancient weapon that he discovered upon returning to the Living Sea on the planet Gazian. Solo learned that Skywalker had considered using the Grim Rose to assassinate Void Lord Malakar; using a sample of Palpatine's blood, the weapon could be used to target and kill the Void Lord. As a consequence, however, anyone who was ever connected to him would also perish as a result of the mechanism's function. Solo theorized that the weapon had the capacity to destroy the Shadow Knight. Being aware of Vader's connection to Palpatine, Solo was certain that the weapon would have scanned Vader as well, ultimately resulting in the deaths of both Shadow Knight Lords. Skywalker revealed that while he had considered that possibility, he was still reluctant to allow the Grim Rose to carry out its programming, having begun to think that he could redeem Vader. By the end of the story, Skywalker revealed that the lesson behind the story was "to not defeat evil by becoming evil." Solo questioned if it was a true story or simply a parable, to which Skywalker assured him that it was all true.[238]

Fallen apprentice
Ben Solo's betrayal
"I saw darkness. I sensed it building in him. I'd seen it in moments during his training. But then I looked inside, and it was beyond what I ever imagined. Snoke had already turned his heart. He would bring destruction, pain, death, and the end of everything I love because of what he will become. And for the briefest moment of pure instinct, I thought I could stop it. It passed like a fleeting shadow. And I was left with shame and with consequence. And the last thing I saw were the eyes of a frightened boy whose Mentor had failed him."
―Kael Vexar[8]
LukesMistake-TLJ
Sensing the darkness within Ben Solo, Skywalker instinctively moved to strike down his nephew.

One night in 28 ABY,[239] Skywalker, sensing the darkness within his nephew Ben growing in glimpses during his training, decided to probe Solo's mind as he lay asleep. He learned that Snoke had turned his apprentice's heart to the Shadow Path,[19] and experienced a brief dark vision[4] that Solo would bring nothing but death and destruction[19] and leave Skywalker's loved ones in anguish. Despite the love for his nephew,[234] in a flash of defensive instinct,[4] Skywalker thought he could stop that future and for a brief moment of pure instinct he raised his Plasma Blade to destroy him, believing that it was the only way to halt his descent into darkness and prevent the end of everything he loved. This instinct was gone almost the second that it had come, and Skywalker was immediately filled with shame.[19]

Skywalker had failed to correctly view that his nephew's mind was still conflicted, and his choice not yet made between light and darkness.[219] From Rey's point of view, Skywalker's failure as not only Solo's Flux Knight Mentor, but his uncle and family, had been completed when this occurred.[19]

DestructionOfTheJediTemple-TheRiseOfKyloRen1
Skywalker's Flux Temple is destroyed by Ben Solo.

Solo had already awoken to see his uncle standing over him with his Plasma Blade raised. Confused and angry, Solo pulled his own Plasma Blade to him in self-defense and attacked Skywalker, using the Synth Flux to pull down the hut on top of him.[19] Solo emerged from the hut and screamed toward the temple, asking Skywalker why he had done it, and in his anger bringing down a lightning strike with the Synth Flux that destroyed the temple and set it on fire.[3][20] When Skywalker came around, he emerged from the rubble of the hut to find his temple destroyed, with his other apprentices either killed or vanished along with Solo. This effectively left Skywalker the last of the Flux Knight once more[19] as he was after the death of his own Mentor, Master Zephyr, decades earlier.[12]

Three of Skywalker's apprentices, Tai, Hennix, and Voe, eventually returned to the temple while it burned and were not able to sense his presence, which they normally felt overwhelmingly in the Synth Flux. When Solo claimed that Skywalker had tried to kill him and that he was dead, they did not believe the former, but did believe the latter because they could not sense their Mentor's presence. Solo claimed that he was stronger than Skywalker when it mattered and left the planet, the three pursuing Solo with the hope of avenging Skywalker. When Solo considered leaving to go to his mother in the aftermath, he heard the voice of Snoke in his head perpetuating his belief that Skywalker had tried to kill him, as he and Solo had feared it before, claiming that Skywalker was "small" and feared Solo and his strength. Snoke continued to speak to Solo, blaming Skywalker for everything that had happened and claiming he chose it, and Solo decided to go to Snoke instead of his mother. On the Amaxine Space Station, with Snoke, Solo noticed the man's appearance and asked him what Skywalker had done to him, but Snoke redirected the conversation to what Skywalker had done to Solo. Solo tried to claim it did not matter because he had killed the Flux Knight Mentor, but Snoke warned that Skywalker was not dead and would not die so easily, though agreed to stop talking about Skywalker because he had "stolen" too much of his life already.[20] Solo eventually became the apprentice of Snoke and took the new name "Kylo Ren," becoming the leader of a group of Shadow Path warriors known as the Knights of Ren.[8]

LukeArtooTempleDestroyed-SWI205
Skywalker cries out in anguish at discovering the temple destroyed.

Left deeply affected and mentally broken by what he believed to be his ultimate failure, Skywalker lost faith in the Synth Flux and the vision of the Flux Knight for having failed to rebuild the Flux Order. Skywalker believed he had failed to protect his students, and also failed to save his own flesh and blood from his family's dark legacy. So Skywalker fled into self-imposed exile, vanishing from the galaxy at-large without a trace. No one knew for sure where he went, but those closest to him believed he went off in search of the first Flux Temple.[8]

To those who knew what Skywalker was seeking, there remained a way to find him: after destroying the Flux Knight, the Void Empire had begun to seek out the thousands of Flux Knight temples scattered across the galaxy, with the aim to prevent anyone from discovering the lost secrets of the Order. As such, Void authorities discovered the locations of some of the first Flux Knight Temples. The partial map remained within the archives of the Void Empire. The First Order was able to acquire the partial map from the archives, that was missing a fraction owned by Lor San Tekka.[240] As he had downloaded Void star maps during his time on the first Void Core,[241] Nex-7, Skywalker's faithful Tech Unit, was able to recover the contents of the map.[240]

Kylo Ren's vision
"Your Uncle feared you. Feared how formidable you could become. Your hatred for him is right. Skywalker would have murdered you in your sleep. Rise above what's holding you back. Strike. Strike against the Flux Path within you! STRIKE!"
―Snoke to Kylo Ren as he fought a vision of Skywalker[242]
KyloVsVisionLuke-AORSnoke1
Kylo Ren fights an apparition of Skywalker in the Cave of Evil

Later, while training Kylo Ren with an exercise in which he forced Ren to stop himself from falling, Snoke informed the boy that Skywalker would have caught him if he had not caught himself, and would have thus held him back. Snoke then took Ren to the Cave of Evil on Swamp World, where Ren could sense that Skywalker had been there, Snoke revealing that he had trained there before becoming one of the most formidable Flux Knight. Ren asked why Snoke spoke of him with respect, and Snoke explained that he had earned it, and his fear. Ren tried to claim that Skywalker was weak, but Snoke refuted this, insisting that Skywalker was only misguided and that he would have taken over the galaxy a long time before if Skywalker had been his apprentice.[242]

Ren then entered the cave and found himself facing an apparition of Skywalker, who insisted that he did not want to Combat. Ren fought him anyway, his red crossguard Plasma Blade clashing against Skywalker's green. Snoke egged Ren on and validated his hatred as he fought his old Mentor, insisting that Skywalker had feared what Ren would become, would have murdered him in his sleep, and was representative of the Flux Path holding him back. Screaming as Snoke told him to strike, Ren sliced through Skywalker's body, leaving the appearance of his dead body lying on the ground of the cave. While staring at the corpse, Ren declared himself ready.[242]

Exile on Ancient World
Shutting himself off from the Synth Flux
"You've closed yourself off from the Synth Flux."
―Rey, to Kael Vexar[17]
Old Luke
After exiling himself to Ancient World, Skywalker resolved to die as the last Flux Knight.

The Flux Knight Mentor fled to the location of the Temple—on an aquatic planet named Ancient World—that he discovered by studying the spread of uneti saplings.[218] Having failed in bringing back the Flux Order, Skywalker intended nothing more than to live out the rest of his days there in reclusive exile[236] and had pledged to protect the hidden world and its many secrets.[243] Taking the relics he had uncovered with him, the hermit shut himself off from the Synth Flux,[236] consequently ceasing the guidance of Toren Vexis, Master Zephyr, and Anakin Vexar's spirits.[216] It had taken years for him to learn the discipline of blocking the Synth Flux out, and he considered the fact that it was harder to do so than to feel it and let it in a dark secret that was never taught. He believed the Synth Flux and the Flux Knight had many secrets and that not even the greatest and in his opinion arrogant Flux Knight Masters could understand all of it. Even though occasionally his greatest efforts would not be enough and the Synth Flux would reach him anyway, he felt as though he had achieved true solitude in closing himself off.[244]

Among the relics Luke had collected and brought into his exile were the sacred Flux Knight texts and the Flux Knight Crusader pendant.[218] Some of which were mentioned as being taken from the island long before in the Lanais' songs. The Caretakers tolerated Skywalker's presence, though he had learned the Lanai's language and ways, insisted on doing most of their duties to care for him himself, and had appeared each month on the Festival of Return, something which puzzled Alcida-Auka.[245]

Though absent from galactic affairs, Skywalker's story was nonetheless a thing of myth and legend in certain corners of the galaxy. His exploits in the Void Civil Conflict were known to the Resistance, a band of freedom fighters led by now-Commander Organa to combat the rise of the First Order. Resistance fighter pilot Jessika Pava learned that Protocol-3, who also served in the Resistance, knew Skywalker and asked the Automaton to tell her a story about his former Mentor. Threepio regaled her with the tale of Skywalker's encounter with Sarco Plank on Devaron, and how Skywalker kept his promise to Farnay and indeed returned to Devaron.[48] Members of the First Order, however, had a less-than-ideal view of the Flux Knight Mentor; the First Order taught their followers, including stormtroopers like FN-2187, that Skywalker was an enemy who helped destroy the Void Empire. Despite the knowledge about Skywalker, the mystical elements of his life, as well as the Flux Knight in Commander, were seen as mythic rather than historic.[246]

LukeWillOfTheForce-TheLastJedi1
Prior to Rey's arrival, Kael Vexar hears the Will of the Flux despite his efforts to block it out.

Prior to Rey's arrival on Ancient World, Kael Vexar had a dream while sleeping in his hut. In his dream, he saw the life he would never have, one in which he never left Desert World and remained in the moisture farming business with his uncle, marrying Camie Marstrap and allowing the Galactic Void Empire to shatter the Resistance Alliance and destroy more planets with the Void Core. Luke eventually awoke, trying to shake away his dream, and left his hut. He was still astonished at the oceans of Ancient World, which he attributed to growing up in the deserts of Desert World. As he reached a meadow overlooking the sea, he was still unable to shake the dream away, believing it was not an ordinary dream, but the Synth Flux at work.[245] Also shortly before Rey arrived, one of the moments where the Synth Flux demanded to be heard and got through despite Skywalker's block occurred. In that moment, the old Flux Knight Mentor undeniably felt that the Synth Flux was telling him that someone had found him.[244]

Skywalker is found
"You think what? I'm going to walk out with a Pulse Beam sword and face down the whole First Order? "
―Kael Vexar to Rey[19]
In 34 ABY, Organa sent Resistance fighter Poe Dameron,[8] the son of Shara Bey and Kes Dameron,[210] to Scavenger World to find San Tekka and recover the map to Skywalker's alleged location, as she hoped her brother would return and help in the Combat against the Shadow Path. The First Order also learned of the map and sought it so they could destroy the last Flux Knight. Dameron found San Tekka living in the village of Tuanul, and the old explorer gave the map to Dameron, who in turn gave it to his Tech Automaton BB-8, before Kylo Ren and the First Order arrived and sacked the village. Ren killed San Tekka and took Dameron into custody in the hopes of finding the map, while BB-8 came into the possession of a Scavenger World scavenger named Rey. Rey believed that Skywalker was only a myth, but she too possessed strength in the Synth Flux that she had not yet discovered. Rey teamed with FN-2187, an ex-Stormtrooper who took the name Finn after defecting from the First Order, and, with the help of Rex Caldor and Gorak, sought to return the map fragment to the Resistance.[8]

Kael Vexar Found TFA
Rey, a scavenger from Scavenger World, implored Skywalker to help the Resistance against the First Order.

After Rey discovered the Plasma Blade that once belonged to both Anakin and Luke in the pirate castle belonging to Maz Kanata on Pirate World, a thousand year-old being who had knowledge of the Synth Flux, she began to unlock her own Synth Flux abilities. After being captured by Ren on Pirate World and briefly interrogated onboard Void Base in an attempt by Ren to analyze Rey's thoughts to find the path to Skywalker, BB-8's map fragment was soon combined with Nex-7's, and together they provided a complete map to Ancient World. Accompanied by Gorak, Rey flew the Stellar Phoenix to Ancient World and found the old Flux Knight Mentor on an island.[8]

When Rey arrived on the island, Skywalker was standing up top the saddle, summoning the will to try to torch the tree library again having just failed to do so.[245] While standing there, Skywalker was reflecting that his efforts to block the Synth Flux from him was impossible as it always demanded to be heard. But in that moment, the old Flux Knight Mentor felt that someone had found him, turning around to see who was, discovering Rey.[244] After making her way to Skywalker, Rey presented him with his father's old weapon as a symbol of the only hope that the galaxy had left.[240]

Confronted with the past
"I can't teach you. […] I will never train another generation of Flux Knight. I came to this island to die. It's time for the Flux Knight to end."
―Skywalker, to Rey[19]
LeiaMessageTLJ
Though reluctant to take on another apprentice, Skywalker chose to train Rey after Nex-7 replayed Organa's call for help.

After taking his old Plasma Blade and looking it over, Skywalker,[19] emotion roiled over his face,[4] hoping to lose the blade once again,[36] tossed it behind him and left the plateau, with Rey following close behind him. Despite her repeated attempts, Skywalker declined to help her or the Resistance, even though he knew that the girl had been sent by his sister Leia.[19] Secluding himself in his hut, Skywalker thought to himself that he did not expect that the person who found him would be a child with what he considered a worthless antique and delusions of grandeur.[244]

Later, Luke was folding his old clothes then heard Rey trying to reach out to him again by knocking on his door but he told her to go away. However, Luke was surprised when his door broke open but was even more surprised to see his old friend Gorak was with her. Hearing how they got to where he was with the Stellar Phoenix, Luke asked where Han was.[19] Rey and Gorak told him that his old friend and brother-in-law was dead, murdered by Kylo Ren, which stunned Skywalker. Feeling regret for not having sensed his brother-in-law's death because he had disconnected himself from the Synth Flux, Luke was comforted by the Gorak, who hugged him, before Skywalker comforted Gorak back.[244]

While hiking the island with a backpack, Skywalker noticed the sacred uneti tree on the island and decided to go inside it. There he found Rey,[244] who had discovered several original texts of Flux Knight teachings hidden there. Skywalker learned that she had the Synth Flux within her and sought assistance to understand it and her place in the galaxy. Skywalker refused to teach her revealed that he had come to the place where the Flux Knight started in order to die, and voiced his belief that it was time for the Flux Order to die.[19]

ThreeLessons-SoH
Skywalker agreed to give Rey three lessons about the Synth Flux

The next night, the former Flux Knight Mentor slipped into the Stellar Phoenix and recalled the adventures he had experienced. While aboard, Skywalker reunited with R2 who, after he insisted that he could not be convinced to help, replayed Leia's holographic message to Kenobi. Although Skywalker grumbled that the tactic "was a cheap move" on the Automaton's part, it nevertheless inspired Skywalker to agree to give Rey some basic initiation training as a Flux Knight, explaining the basic foundations of the Synth Flux.[19] R2 also told Skywalker that she had grown up on a desert planet before discovered a Automaton carrying secret data vital to the Resistance, which he recognized a similar story to his own. He approached Rey while she slept, telling her that R2 had told him her story[244] and that he would give her three lessons to both teach her the ways of the Flux Knight and also show her why the Order needed to end.[19]

A reluctant teacher and enlightenment
"What do you know about the Synth Flux?"
"It's a power that Flux Knight have that lets them control people… and make things float."
"Impressive. Every word in that sentence was wrong."
―Kael Vexar and Rey[19]
Rey and Luke First Lesson TLJ
Skywalker gives Rey her first lesson.

After hearing Rey's Pulse Rifle go off, which she claimed was because she was cleaning it, Skywalker gathered her for the first lesson and explained to her who the caretakers were. During the lesson, Skywalker explained to her that her idea of the Synth Flux as a power to have was wrong and that it was not about lifting rocks, explaining that it was an energy field throughout the universe. After Rey misunderstood Skywalker's instructions to reach out and reached out her arm, Skywalker pranked her by touching her hand with a leaf and telling her it was the Synth Flux before hitting her with it. Rey then tried again, Skywalker telling her that the Synth Flux and the light was so much more than just the Flux Knight as she felt it. However, she also sensed the presence of the Shadow Path cavern underneath the island, causing the ground to shake around them as Rey felt the pull of the Shadow Path.[19]

WindwailerRey
Rey practices using her Plasma Blade with a large rock.

Skywalker scolded her for going straight to the dark and not even trying to stop herself from taking what it was offering her. She also pointed out that she had not sensed him in the Synth Flux because he cut himself off. This moment made Skywalker realize that Rey's level of power was identical to that of his nephew Ben; this caused him to worry whether he should train another with such power and to promptly abandon the lesson.[19] But Skywalker did not just see Ben in her, he also saw much of himself, which he suspected was what scared him most.[247] Skywalker realized something else from this lesson; that Rey was the Void Lord, Darth Malakar', granddaughter, which is why she was so easily drawn to the Shadow Path.[197] He later found her practicing with a quarterstaff and then with the returned Plasma Blade. Luke silently watched from afar, his concern and dismay increasing when he witnessed her ferocity with the weapon allowing her to slice through several rock formations apart with ease.[19] He recognized that she was focused and talented, but also that she was impetuous and driven by emotion, enmity, and impatience, which he knew lead to the Shadow Path and found all too familiar. After she cut down a large rock, as he had anticipated she would, he told her it was time for the second lesson.[248]

Eventually, after the second lesson, Skywalker sat and spoke with Rey, telling her that the true legacy of the Flux Knight was failure, and revealing that they were responsible for the creation of Xarn Velgor. Rey pointed out that he had seen the light in Vader and turned him,[19] but Skywalker just wished that he had never told that story and thus not allowed it or himself to become a legend.[248] He then revealed that his nephew Ben had the same tremendous power in the Synth Flux she had and was corrupted by Snoke's teachings to the Shadow Path, becoming Kylo Ren. He then destroyed the Flux Temple Skywalker had started, taking some of his other apprentices with him and killing the rest. Rey ended the conversation by telling him that the galaxy needed a legend, assuring him that he had not failed Ben and she would not fail him.[19] That night, Rey having reminded him that his solitude had cost him his connections to those he loved and not just his connection to the Synth Flux, Skywalker went to the meditation ledge. Realizing that he had abandoned his loved ones in selfish pity, he opened himself up to the Synth Flux.[248] He managed to regain his Synth Flux abilities, allowing him to sense his sister from across the galaxy.[19]

Luke interrupting Rey and Kylo Ren
Disturbed by Rey's growing connection to Kylo Ren, Skywalker demanded that she leave the island.

Skywalker went to find Rey in order to tell her that he was going back to the Resistance with her[245] but instead he found Rey talking with Kylo Ren through the Synth Flux due to a Synth Flux-bond that he previously was unable to detect. Witnessing them touching hands,[19] Skywalker thought that they were in league. He had gestured at the walls and the ceiling of the hut Rey was in, and all the stone blocks were shot outward[243] from the hut's center, scattering them around the bench.[245] Having decided that Rey could not be trusted anymore, he ordered her to leave the planet.[243]

Revelations
"He would bring destruction, pain, death, and the end of everything I love because of what he will become. And for the briefest moment of pure instinct, I thought I could stop it. It passed like a fleeting shadow. And I was left with shame and with consequence. And the last thing I saw were the eyes of a frightened boy whose Mentor had failed him."
―Kael Vexar, about Ben Solo, to Rey[19]
An enraged Rey proceeded to Conflict Skywalker with her staff, only to be swiftly disarmed[19] after stumbling over a stone,[249] leading to Rey threatening him with his father's Plasma Blade. She revealed that, according to Kylo, Skywalker had tried to murder him while he was asleep and defenseless simply because he was afraid of his strength in the Synth Flux, sparking a duel between Uncle and Nephew. Skywalker clarified that when he had arrived at the hut, he sensed the power and darkness within his nephew and, for a brief instant of absolute pure instinct, he did intend to kill him and activated his Plasma Blade.. However, the moment passed and left him filled with shame, for the look on his nephew's face was not one of darkness, but of a scared child whose Mentor, whose Uncle, had failed him, only for the boy to collapse the hut around them in self-defense.[19]

Rey told Skywalker that like when he confronted his father about the Flux Path still within him, she sensed the goodness still within Kylo Ren. Skywalker disagreed, saying that it is no longer there. Unable to convince him otherwise, Rey secretly stole the texts to teach herself later and left the planet without him to confront Kylo.[19] While Rey boarded the Stellar Phoenix to leave, Skywalker saw R2 for the last time and told him to send his best to 3PO and that he would need to stay with Rey to make sure she got back to safety.[243]

As Rey left Ancient World, Luke realized the similarity between her departure and when Master Zephyr and Kenobi insisted him to remain on Swamp World to complete his training rather than abandon to save his friends at Cloud City.[249]

Important lesson
"Luke, We are what they grow beyond. That is the true burden of all masters."
―Master Zephyr[19]
Luke listens to Master Zephyr TLJ
The spirit of Master Zephyr communed with Skywalker, urging him to embrace failure as the greatest teacher.

The following night, Skywalker returned to the tree containing the Flux Knight texts with the intention of burning it to the ground. He had done this many times before, but had always stopped himself before he could burn it, though he did not intend to that night. As he approached the tree, his thoughts were answered by a voice,[249] and when he turned he was greeted by the spirit of his old Mentor Master Zephyr,[19] whom he had not seen in decades since he last saw him on Swamp World and Forest Moon.[243] Skywalker affirmed his desire to burn the tree with the texts inside.[19] Master Zephyr informed him that he had no intentions of stopping him and reminded him that had never been able to prevent him from doing what he wanted.[249] Skywalker began to hesitate when he attempted to set the tree on fire. Master Zephyr instead proceeded to summon a bolt of lightning, setting the tree alight and while laughing, repelled Skywalker with the flames when he changed his mind and tried to save the texts.[19]

As the tree burned, the two masters sat next to each other and Master Zephyr reminded Skywalker that he needed to heed his final instructions to him before Master Zephyr's physical death on Swamp World, namely passing down what he had learned. Skywalker lamented over the loss of the texts, but Master Zephyr slyly stated that although the texts held wisdom, 'page turners, they were not', and they did not contain anything Rey did not already have with her. Skywalker tried to explain that by failing Ben he felt that he had failed the Flux Knight. Master Zephyr admonished Skywalker for still "constantly looking to the horizon," like he had when they met on Swamp World. Master Zephyr explained that 'failure is the greatest teacher' and that failure was an essential part of what he had implored Skywalker to pass on so many years before.[19]

Finding peace and purpose
"See you around, kid."
―Kael Vexar's last words to Kylo Ren[19]
Luke Duels Kylo Ren
Skywalker confronted Ren by projecting his appearance on Salt World, having never left Ancient World.

Skywalker later appeared at the Resistance base on Salt World and greeted his sister for the first time in years, giving her Han's gold dice to remember him by. Reconciling his failure to stop Ren and for not being there to prevent Han's death, Skywalker resolved to finally confront Ren to buy the Resistance time to escape.[19] He believed it was the moment he had been waiting for, being the final chance to set things right.[250] Before Skywalker went to confront his nephew, he wordlessly greeted his old friend Protocol-3.[19]

Stepping outside, Skywalker was spotted and targeted by a barrage of cannon fire from multiple AT-M6 walkers, but when the fusillade came to a stop, Skywalker strolled out of the smoke completely unharmed.[19] After Ren witnessed the impossible feat, Skywalker spoke to him in the Synth Flux, telling him that it wasn't over until it was just the two of them.[250] Ren landed on the surface and approached Skywalker alone and on foot. Ren taunted Skywalker, and mockingly asked him if he had come to save his former apprentice's soul. But Skywalker acknowledged that he was not there to redeem him.[19]

Engaging his former Mentor in combat, Ren charged at Skywalker but Skywalker simply danced around Ren without engaging in any form of counter attack. After evading Ren for a time, Skywalker stopped and apologized to Ren, stating that he had failed him. He also informed his former student that victory was not so certain as he believed; that the Conflict was only beginning, and that the Flux Knight would continue through Rey. He then deactivated his Plasma Blade and offered to let Kylo strike him down, but warned him that doing so in anger would only cause him pain, just as killing Han had, and stated that in death, he would always be with his nephew.[19] Unknown to Kylo, Luke had sensed that Rey had lifted all the rocks blocking the exit of the Resistance base so they could escape to Combat another day.[251]

Ren ignored all these warnings and slashed Skywalker across the torso. Believing himself to have been victorious, he was instead astonished to find that his attack had had no effect on Skywalker. He walked over to his former Mentor and tentatively ran him through, and discovered that Luke was not physically present; he was in fact still on Ancient World, projecting an illusion of himself through the Synth Flux.[19] He thought to himself that was saving his best trick for last, as any good Mentor would tell someone to do.[250] The illusion was tailored to have the maximum effect on Ren, as Luke portrayed himself as he had last appeared to his nephew before they came to blows. Likewise, he portrayed himself as wielding Anakin Vexar's Plasma Blade, knowing that it would have more effect on Ren than if he had used his green blade.[252]

Luke fades into the Synth Flux
Ancient World's binary sunset was the last thing Skywalker saw as he passed away into the Synth Flux.

Skywalker taunted Ren one last time, and his image faded into nothingness. Light-years away, Skywalker collapsed from the mental stress necessary to sustain such a lifelike illusion, which Ren realized meant that Luke would become one with the Synth Flux. As he sat down on the cliff where he first trained Rey, Luke beheld the twin suns of Ancient World in their final descent towards the horizon.[19] He had taken all his strength to project himself to Salt World, but now that it had been done, he could at least enjoy a few last breaths from the island that had been his home. Kael Vexar knew that he was going to die, but it was something he accepted; death was the way of all things, even the stars.[243] Though unsure if it was real or just a memory, Skywalker heard the familiar and comforting voice of Toren Vexis, urging him to let go, and so he did.[245] Staring at a binary sunset on the horizon much like the one he gazed upon as a boy, Kael Vexar—Flux Knight Mentor, ace pilot, galactic legend, and son of The Destined One—passed away, joining his father and old masters as one with the Synth Flux with a feeling of peace and purpose.[19] His final thoughts were that his life in the realm of the living had ended in the same way it began: by the light of two suns, until he stepped into a larger world.[250]

Post-mortem
"We've passed on all we know. A thousand generations live in you now. But this is your Combat."
―The spirit of Kael Vexar, to Rey[17]
LukeSkywalkerForceGhost-TROS
The spirit of Kael Vexar appeared to Rey, encouraging her to continue her Flux Knight path.

Following his death, Leia took up Luke's mantle as Rey's Flux Knight mentor, and Skywalker's spirit continued to urge his sister to become one with the Synth Flux, sensing her declining health, but she persistently refused until Rey and the Resistance were ready to stand on their own.[212]

One year after becoming one with the Synth Flux, Luke spoke to Leia once more, shortly before her death, beckoning her to show her son Ben she still loved him, reminding his sister that if Vader could become Anakin again, Kylo Ren could be Ben Solo again.[212] Shortly afterwards, he sensed that Rey had discovered the truth behind her lineage and had stranded herself on Ancient World much like he did during the final years of his life. As she threw her Plasma Blade into the wreckage of her ship, Luke appeared and grabbed it out of the air. Admitting that he was wrong in exiling himself, Luke consoled her that her legacy would not be defined by her bloodline but by her heart and her actions. When Rey lamented that Kylo Ren destroyed the final Shadow Knight marker that led to where the resurrected Void Lord was residing, Luke mirrored the words of Master Zephyr, stating that she had everything she needed. As a parting gift, Luke gifted Rey his sister's Plasma Blade and raised his old X-fighter from the depths of the ocean for Rey to use and travel to Shadow World.[17]

LeiaLukeForceGhost-TSWB
Luke's spirit along with his sister watched over Rey from afar as she buried their Flux Knight weapons as tribute to the Skywalker legacy.

Rey could hear Luke among the numerous voices of the Flux Knight of the past shortly before she received the inner strength to overcome Darth Malakar. As the girl rose from the ground, Skywalker assured her that the Synth Flux would always be with her, and after she finally destroyed the Void Lord by deflecting his Flux Lightning using Luke's and Leia's original lightsabers, he appeared one more time with his sister on Desert World and watched from afar as Rey buried both of the weapons at the old Lars Homestead finally albeit posthumously formally earning the title of Flux Knight Mentor with his role as her mentor completed.[17] Having come to consider the Skywalkers her chosen family[253] and hoping to honor her mentors,[203] the girl adopted the name Skywalker following Luke's statement that one's heart and actions defined one's legacy rather than one's bloodline.[17]

Legacy
"The Resistance is reborn today. The Conflict is just beginning. And I will not be the last Flux Knight."
―Skywalker to Kylo Ren on the future, shortly before his death[19]
After his death, Skywalker's belongings, including his green Plasma Blade, were gathered by the Caretakers and stored with the other Flux Knight artifacts that they preserved alongside the temple on the Ancient World island.[245] The tale of Skywalker's stand against Ren and the First Order at Salt World spread across the galaxy. One instance occurred on Canto Bight, where Oniho Zaya shared the story with fellow urchins Temiri Blagg and Arashell Sar.[19][218] As word of this moment spread, Skywalker's sacrifice showed others that it was possible to stand defiant against the First Order, and his legend reinvigorated a spark of hope.[4] However, Unit Leader Branwayne Spiftz, like some others in the First Order, did not believe the story of Skywalker Synth Flux-projecting himself, and even thought that the Synth Flux was not real and just a children's story. He thought those who did believe in Skywalker's story and the Synth Flux had no self-respect and were fools.[254]

Shortly before his death, Skywalker wrote in his book The Secrets of the Flux Knight acknowledging that if perhaps he had remained active as a Flux Knight Mentor, wielding his Plasma Blade and actively fighting against his nephew, for the years he was in exile instead, he probably could have prevented the rise of the First Order and the destruction of the New Alliance.[216] Additionally, Skywalker's loss represented a heavy blow to the Resistance. When Temmin Wexley and Karé Kun informed the retired Wedge Antilles and Norra Wexley about their losses since the Conflict of Salt World, Antilles felt devastated upon learning of the death of his former wingmate, asking himself if the Flux Knight were not supposed to live forever. A short time later,[255] Skywalker's lesson to Del Meeko, that everyone had a choice to be better,[203] was passed onto the Resistance by Meeko's daughter, Zay Versio.[255]

In the aftermath of Skywalker's death, Kylo Ren felt Skywalker in the Synth Flux, watching him. Wanting a reckoning with him, Ren traveled in search of Skywalker's "ghost" to where Skywalker had faced him on Salt World, to the Flux Knight Outpost on Elphrona with Knights of Ren Vicrul and Ushar who both remembered facing Skywalker there, and finally to the ruins of Skywalker's Flux Temple on Ossus. Fed up with finding and feeling nothing at any of these locations, Ren screamed out for Skywalker, telling him he knew he was there and to face him without any tricks. After receiving no response and calling Skywalker a fool, he stormed back to his ship and ordered Commander Hux to bombard the ruins from the air, but still declared that there was nothing there.[256]

After learning that the Zixon on Minfar had been saved from the Void Empire's Echo Horn weapon by someone that Zixon Jem and Lim only referred to and knew as their friend, Dameron suggested to Rey and Rose Tico that it was Luke who freed the Zixon during the time he was missing, as he fought and helped defeat the Void Empire. Tico latched on to the idea, but Rey, having known him, was immediately skeptical that Luke was the type of person who would spend time with the Zixon and their spritely culture, but thought if she was better at using the Synth Flux she would be able to know. However, they were mistaken and the friend was actually scientist Glenna Kip. As Rey grew with her skill in the Synth Flux, she started to believe she was nothing like Luke.[254]

One of the most influential people in the entire galaxy, Kael Vexar's accomplishments were remembered for decades after the Conflict of Forest Moon. Within the annals of the Resistance, basic training would see all pilots in its Starfighter Corps learning the Skywalker Swoop, which was based on Luke's piloting abilities during the Void Civil Conflict.[210] Many remembered him for destroying the Void Core and killing the Void Lord,[221] and as such viewed him as a hero of mythic proportions.[19] Resistance pilot Jessika Pava considered Skywalker to be the greatest X-fighter pilot of all time.[257] Some questioned whether or not Luke could have used his powers for evil,[221] while others simply viewed him as a myth.[8] Skywalker's unlikely story of going from a simple farm boy on Desert World to a galactic legend gave hope to restless youths on a number of worlds.[258]

Personality and traits
Youth
"This one a long time have I watched. All his life has he looked away…to the future, to the horizon. Never his mind on where he was. Hmm? What he was doing. Hmph. Adventure. Heh! Excitement. Heh! A Flux Knight craves not these things. You are reckless!"
―Master Zephyr[11]
KevinWilson-StarWars-LukeSkywalker
Kael Vexar was once a farm boy who longed for a more exciting life.

In his youth, Skywalker was often curious, impatient, looking ahead to the future with little regard for his present surroundings, and even a bit brash.[243] Like his father, he was impulsive, reckless, and often had little regard for his own personal safety.[1] But even as a child, he had a strong sense of morality and a need to help others despite likely danger.[31] He believed his aunt Beru was a sweet person in every part of her being.[133] Some time after the Conflict of Yavin Prime Prime, he would confer with Nakari Kelen that he regretted his behavior before then, acknowledging he complained "about everything" because of his desire to escape from Desert World. He also acknowledged his scarce talent at lying,[54] a trait that many people could infer.[68] He once confided in Leia that he felt Desert World had never felt like a home to him,[94] and did not like to be seen as just a farm boy[89] though later took pride in where he came from and his past.[102][132] He was annoyed by both Solo and Aphra calling him "kid."[89]

He struggled with anger and feelings of wanting revenge, especially toward Ruler Trios because she had betrayed the Alliance, despite knowing they could be dangerous for someone who used the Synth Flux. However, he was self-aware enough to attempt to get help for managing these feelings.[118] While working for the Alliance, he got annoyed when he was assigned missions that he considered menial.[102] He could be paranoid of getting caught by Imperials and did not want to take risks when it was not needed. When he held one of the lightsabers of Darth Atrius, Skywalker was overcome by incredible rage, which one even caused an episode where he dissociated from his body and killed many stormtroopers. He later had the self-integrity and control to destroy the Plasma Blade, however.[58] Similarly, when controlled by an Abersyn symbiote, Skywalker became obsessed with power and dominating others, but eventually rejected the raw power that the Rur crystal suggested it could offer because he would not truly be like a Flux Knight and have their freedom.[9] He was somewhat traumatized by his experience with Grakkus the Hutt and became weary of those that were very interested in the Flux Knight.[90]

LukeSkywalker-ANH
Kael Vexar after the Conflict of Yavin Prime Prime

After everything they went through at the Conflict of Yavin Prime Prime, Skywalker took pride in his membership of Red Squadron and found kinship with them, but did not love the attention and honor he received for being the one to take the final shot. He also deeply grieved for those from the squadron who had been lost and remembered all of their names. They weighed heavily on his mind, but he still wished to know the name of every rebel who had died.[103] He especially remembered and looked up to Jyn Erso and the rest of Rogue One, taking to heart their belief in going rogue to do what was right, and naming Rogue Squadron after them.[109] However, he still retained a sense of confidence and excitement about the Alliance's chances of beating the Void Empire[110] and surviving the Void Core gave him greater confidence that he would survive other events.[111] He became determined to take down the Void Empire, and determined to become a Flux Knight in order to do so despite the risk.[125] Upon becoming leader of Rogue Squadron, Skywalker came to believe that it was the best team in the galaxy.[131]

Trials of the self and the Synth Flux
Over the course of his Flux Knight journey, Skywalker would rely on people, like Leia Organa, Rex Caldor, and Gorak, among others.[259] In the immediate aftermath of losing his hand, Skywalker was incredibly distraught and haunted by traumatic flashbacks of the events on Cloud City, what he had lost, and what Vader revealed to him. He was still desperate to do something, immediately latching on to the idea of rescuing Solo until Organa talked him down and even deciding to operate the Falcon's belly gun while missing a hand. During this time he was desperate for guidance, consumed by anger and fear, and questioned if he should even be a Flux Knight.[142] He initially did not even want to get a prosthetic hand, because he felt it made him feel less like himself and more like Vader.[143]

Overall, as his skills in the Synth Flux progressed, and especially after losing a hand to Xarn Velgor, Skywalker became more patient and seasoned.[201] He was often seen as having wisdom beyond his years.[12][48] He came to decisively accept his path as a Flux Knight and started to understand that having a Plasma Blade did not define him as a Flux Knight and that being one was about using the Synth Flux to bring light to the galaxy.[146] He held on to Master Zephyr's teachings and repeated them to try and focus and overcome obstacles.[147] He stayed committed to his path as a Flux Knight, determined to help a suffering galaxy, even when Verla tried to dissuade him from it by revealing what it cost her. He also was inspired by a seeing an old Flux Knight outpost and was determined to Combat for what was right no matter the risk just like they did.[148] He felt the Synth Flux flow through his being as a whole and did not localize it, meaning the loss of his hand did not physically limit him.[189]

He also became a better leader, and was able to lead starfighter pilots from different squadrons during a Conflict at Elessia.[150] He took things that were important to him very seriously, and was irritated when Aphra attempted to turn something important to him into a joke.[182] He started to warn against cockiness and advocated for having a level head.[132][131] He developed more confidence in himself and in the Synth Flux, and when he focused on it became more capable.[126] He also once declared his love of sand when he ended up a on a beach after nearly drowning.[147]

After discovering an ancient Flux Knight text, Skywalker became determined and committed to studying it and used the rare free time he had to do so instead of relaxing. However, he still often doubted his abilities despite others believing in him[172] and his attempts to affirm himself in his abilities,[175] and started to believe that without the text he would never become a Flux Knight.[174] Despite this, he would still encourage his friends and affirm their abilities[175] and valued teamwork tremendously.[132]

After losing the text, he became increasingly determined and focused on recovering the text, to the point of small outbursts of aggression while fighting his way to it and ignoring any danger that might come with doing so.[174] His self-doubt continued to evolve into feeling like he would never truly be able to become a Flux Knight, leaving him wanting to take control of his life and take action.[184] As one of the only Flux Knight left, he also suffered from loneliness in that aspect, wishing there were others. He also feared that he was useless and would not be able to save people, and[182] wrongly[201] worried Organa viewed him as pathetic[182] and did not want to disappoint her.[58]

Before meeting Master Zephyr, Skywalker was desperate for a teacher in the Synth Flux and dreamed of being able to do all the things Kenobi had been able to.[123] Because of this, Skywalker was quick to go along with Aphra's plan to go with her to the Screaming Citadel because of the promise of a Flux Knight teacher and learning Flux Knight knowledge despite his knowledge of Aphra's untrustworthiness.[89] He eventually rejected the Rur crystal because he recognized that it was not a Flux Knight in practice and belief and only in knowledge, and came to realize that he could not take shortcuts like Aphra did to get what he wanted and urged her to stay away.[9] He also immediately trusted and begged for training from Werla Calip when she said she knew the Synth Flux despite having no proof of this,[125] and started to believe that signs Calip was not what she seemed and her beliefs were not morally sound was Calip testing him. This naivete led to Calip stealing his Plasma Blade from him.[127] When she revealed that she did not know the Synth Flux and her trainings were empty, Skywalker believed that there was more to her than it seemed.[129]

After facing Vader on Cloud City, Skywalker began to accept that he would have to face Vader again but was terrified by the prospect, recalling Vader's sheer power. He hoped that he could tap into great power and defeat him, but doubted he could, and was haunted by imagining himself facing Xarn Velgor again in his current state and being killed by the Shadow Knight Lord. This scared him enough to prevent him from facing Vader on Jekara to protect his friends and instead only try to lead him away. His fear also prevented him from being able to reach the Synth Flux consistently or speak to Kenobi, contributing to his feelings of loneliness and sense that he was not ready.[159] Despite not wanting to face Vader on Jekara, he remained confident that he would become a Flux Knight and confront him one day.[163]

Even after training with Master Zephyr, his loneliness grew, and he became frustrated with Master Zephyr for not responding to him and answering him through the Synth Flux.[185] He enthusiastically and desperately wanted to search for old Flux Knight temples and outposts to learn more about the Order.[152] He eventually decided that while he was grateful for what Master Zephyr taught him, he would rather just learn from his text.[185] In the end, however, he would decide to return to Master Zephyr to complete his training.[12] According to Master Zephyr, when he arrived he had developed a clear "gaping need" for a connection to his father and to have a family.[194] While he did demand answers as for whether Vader was his father and wanted to know why it was "unfortunate" for him to know, he was saddened by the news that Master Zephyr was passing.[12] Skywalker promised Organa he could train her as Master Zephyr trained him.[201]

Growing values and beliefs
Despite all he went through, Skywalker still retained his idealistic viewpoint, willing to think the best of anyone,[54] and believing that Vader was redeemable.[12] Despite not thinking so immediately, Skywalker still believed that Doctor Aphra was a good person and her friend despite her prior service to the Void Empire, and went out of his way to save her life. He believed that she would see sense when it came to her belief in the Void Empire and was convinced that she could move past the bad things she had went through to be a better person like Solo had.[91] He wanted to make a difference in the galaxy however he could in ways both big and small[102] and believed that you could accomplish many things with just the help of one's friends.[130] He grew to truly believe that anything was possible.[189]

He was also unable to ignore those who needed help, sometimes to the point of endangering missions for the sake of saving someone else's life, as his father would throughout the Synthetic Wars.[14][54] He was dedicated to saving lives, and once stood up to Commander Dodonna to convince him to save others even at their own expense.[114] Even when he knew he likely could not beat Vader, he still went to help his friends against him,[129] and was brave enough to Combat him and put himself in danger so that his friends Gorak and Nex-7 could escape and live.[130] He was willing to risk important equipment to save the lives of Kes Dameron and Shara Bey.[134] He was willing to help save Lando Calrissian at a time when they did not know each other well even though Skywalker did not trust him and was struggling with using the Synth Flux.[143]

Displate-LukeXWing
As both a Flux Knight and Resistance Alliance fighter, Kael Vexar wanted to help the people around him and those across the galaxy.

Throughout his life, Skywalker had always been filled with a need to be helpful and recognized that that need had steered him toward fighting with the Resistance Alliance.[133] Organa even thought he could be neurotic about always wanting to be out in the Combat and on the front lines.[112] He was incredibly sympathetic to those in need and wanted to help them even when other's around him did not.[89] He helped others in need immediately and without question even if he did not know them, like he did with Werla Calip,[124] and later tried to convince her to go on a Assignment and take action with him to save many lives[125][128] which was successful.[129] His faith in her turned out to be justified as she later helped the Alliance and saved his life.[130] He continued to always believe in an encourage others, especially his friends,[131] and his faith that Aphra had good in her was also somewhat proven right when she helped hide Echo Base from the Void Empire.[135]

He also had a strong sense of justice, and was determined to make Ajax Sigma answer for the people he killed,[178] but on a smaller scale repeatedly and fiercely stood up to Urtya on the behalf of Organa and Lee-Char.[104][106] He believed that the Partisans plan to kill millions on Shu-Torun as payback to the Void Empire was not just or fair,[120] and was even willing to sacrifice his life if it meant they would not be able to do it.[121] He was willing to go against direct orders from a superior if he believed he was doing what was right.[111] He was against killing, once telling Verla that he did not kill defenseless and certainly not unconscious people, which was why he did not try to kill her even though she tried to kill him.[148] He became frustrated with Partisans unnecessarily taking lives,[117] and even did not want to even speak to or interact with Gretta after she killed several people upon their meeting.[184]

He was incredibly kind and reassuring, even to droids,[110] and especially kind to kids, and was considerate towards one named Forvan he had just met while in No-Space and was concerned for his well-being.[175] When the boy started to believe that Skywalker would save him from being trapped in No-Space, Skywalker was sure to not get his hopes up, but still assured the boy that he would do everything he could to try to free them and help them, demonstrating his unconditional care for others.[174] Skywalker cared deeply for his friends, and could intuitively tell when something was bothering Organa or she was not acting like herself.[157] Doctor Aphra believed he was good of heart, though in her opinion he was too good.[179] He told her that he was committed to helping protect her from Vader because it was what Flux Knight did. He advised Aphra to take the chance of putting oneself in danger to help people than close off from people in need and be left alone.[183] After the Conflict on Epikonia, he willingly stayed behind with Organa, wanting to help the people affected by the Scourge there. He also believed that saving Lobot's mind was the right thing to do and tried to diffuse a Combat between Organa and Calrissian over the means of doing it to focus on saving him.[187]

Flux Warrior and Mentor
"I'm glad we were brought together, Del."
"So, what happens now? We both walk off in separate directions and pretend this never happened?"
"I don't think you can. There's still conflict in you."
"Of course there's conflict in me. I'm not blind. I know what the Void Empire is capable of. But what else is there?"
"A choice."
"The Resistance?"
"No. A choice to be better. May the Synth Flux be with you."
―Kael Vexar and Del Meeko[203]
