Leia Organa took note of Skywalker's change into a calmer man as he became a Flux Warrior, thinking of the change in regard to the years that had past: while he was somewhat like other rebels who had joined the Combat hopeful, only for those rebels to become jaded over the course of the Conflict, Organa noticed a much deeper stillness within him. While she knew he was the same person, she did nearly mourn the loss of Skywalker's innocent optimism and excitement. All the same, she saw how forgiving Skywalker was towards their father and pondered how it was possible for him to not hate Vader the way she did; Skywalker tried to tell her about Anakin Vexar's redemption to the light, but she refused to listen. Skywalker was incredibly happy for his sister and Solo's marriage. He also offered to train her in the ways of the Synth Flux, thinking they could be wonderful together as Flux Knight Knights, and gave her a chance to come with him on his hunt for information about the Flux Knight of the past. While he did not see it as Organa needing to chose between a life with Solo or a life as a Flux Knight, Organa did and declined.[201]

Following the end of the Void Empire and the death of the Shadow Order, Skywalker came to have great hope for the future of the Flux Knight, coming to believe that if a single Flux Knight could help bring back Harmony to the Synth Flux, then a restored Flux Order perhaps could maintain that achieved peace and prevent the Shadow Path of the Synth Flux from ever regaining its hold on the galaxy. This belief led Luke to scour worlds for the remaining Flux Knight texts and gathered a new class of apprentices under his tutelage.[216] Furthermore, when he encountered the Void Special Forces trooper Del Meeko, he left the man with the lesson that all he needed to do was be better,[203] a lesson that would be passed onto Meeko's daughter, Zay Versio, and the Resistance.[255]

During his brief time as Grogu's Mentor, Luke had to suppress his more sentimental side and be more professional in an effort to successfully pass on the Flux Knight values to the child.[228] Skywalker became confident in his abilities as a Flux Knight Mentor, all while he became perceived by the galaxy as a legend. He was confident that he could take down seven Knights of Ren on his own, and expressed to them that he was superior at using the Synth Flux than them, even slightly taunting them and predicting that they would leave crying. This confidence was justified, as he did successfully defeat them. He also cared deeply for his nephew Ben Solo, and was happy when Solo joined him on missions. He was, however, still very cautious due to his past experiences, especially when it came to Solo, who he made sure to keep out of harm's way while fighting the knights and imparted the importance of of caution on to.[232]

Later life
"I failed. Because I was Kael Vexar. Flux Knight Mentor. A legend."
"The galaxy may need a legend."
―Kael Vexar and Rey[19]
LukeSkywalkerJediHermit-SWBC43
In his later years, Skywalker lived in exile as a grief-ridden and embittered hermit.

After Ben Solo, his nephew, betrayed him and killed most of his apprentices, Skywalker became embittered as well as grief-ridden and chose to exile himself to the planet Ancient World, where he would spend the rest of his life in with the intention of dying where the Flux Knight began to forever end the Flux Knight.[19] Despite all of this, he still comforted Gorak when he learned of Rex Caldor's death, and became even more riddled with guilt.[244] He also became more cautious in teaching others of immense power due to Solo's betrayal, feeling finished with the Flux Order,[260] as evidenced by when he initially regarded Rey as a delusional child,[244] initially refused to train her, and when he would later decide to abruptly end their lessons after seeing her drawn towards the Shadow Path.[19] Part of this was because he was deeply terrified by how much she reminded him of himself.[247] He also now believed that there was no hope for those who fell to the Shadow Path, as shown when he openly expressed that he believed there was no hope for Kylo Ren to return to the light.[19] By this point, Skywalker had come to regard Ren as his responsibility and his greatest failure.[216] However, Rey helped him realize that in disconnecting himself from the Synth Flux, he had abandoned those he loved in selfish pity, which he was determined to rectify.[248]

However, after Master Zephyr's spirit encouraged him that Rey was the Flux Knight's hope, Skywalker finally realized his wrongs and chose to help the Resistance. Despite his lost faith in his nephew, Skywalker showed great remorse for failing to stop his fall to the Shadow Path, and for himself ending up being the turning point in Solo's fall.[19] Skywalker realized that instead of blaming himself, he blamed the Flux Knight for his failures. His belief of the Flux Knight being romanticized deities with a legacy of failure remained, but gained realization that if one stripped away the sacred temples, lightsabers, and even the legends about them, they were just good people the galaxy needed.[216] In the end, Skywalker willingly sacrificed himself for the Flux Knight to be revived and the Resistance to live on. He passed away on Ancient World shortly after, having made peace with his failures and having no more regrets.[19]

Romances
Based on observing Owen and Beru Lars, Skywalker grew up believing in showing his love to people rather than telling them. When he and Nakari Kelen fell in love, he demonstrated his feelings for her, but he did not tell her how he felt; he regretted that as grieved for her death alongside the then-recent losses of his aunt, uncle, and Toren Vexis.[54] While stranded on Hubin, Skywalker was instantly smitten with Tula Markona[261] and they shared a kiss,[262] but it would be a one-time event. His commitment to the Resistance contributed to the death of her father, and while she told him to carry no blame, she could not look at him without being reminded of that loss.[263] When he mentioned he previously had a brief attraction to Chelli Lona Aphra, Aphra's ex-girlfriend, Sana Starros told him to count himself lucky that Aphra was not interested in men.[89] Skywalker and Reyé Hollis each appreciated the other man's smile and pleasantly-scented hair.[133]

Powers and abilities
Plasma Blade abilities
"I see you have constructed a new Plasma Blade. Your skills are complete."
―Xarn Velgor to Kael Vexar[12]
Return of the Flux Knight 4
Skywalker was an accomplished duelist who triumphed over his father, Xarn Velgor.

Though he received little formal training, Kael Vexar had a natural talent for Plasma Blade combat.[11] Luke's initial training with the weapon of the Flux Knight came during a brief session with Toren Vexis and a training remote on the way to Peace World,[1] where he learned the initial position and the four defensive postures.[48] Though his level proved inadequate during his first, brief duel against Xarn Velgor on Cymoon 1,[63] his combat skills had improved for their duel on Cloud City,[11] and culminated with him besting the Shadow Knight Lord in combat during the Conflict of Forest Moon.[12]

Like most Flux Knight, Skywalker was adept at redirecting Pulse Rifle fire,[1][54][12] and eventually was able to do it while flipping.[126] During his Assignment to Rodia, he tried his hand at Jar'Kai for the first time with his and Huulik's Plasma Blade. Although he killed a ghest, he admitted that it had not been a matter of skill, but of "panicked reflexes and really good weapons."[54] He received further training from the "Gamemaster," a servant of Grakkus the Hutt, who had learned the art of Plasma Blade combat. Forced to Combat him and the Hutt's MagnaGuards, Skywalker improved his cursory abilities.[71] He was able to Combat and defeat Kreel of SCAR Squadron in Plasma Blade combat, with help from utilizing his surroundings,[102] and could easily take down Void droids.[126]

After a brief time of training with Master Zephyr, Skywalker revealed himself to be a gifted duelist on Cloud City. Skywalker engaged Vader in a fierce duel and managed to hold his own for much of the duel, wounding Vader in the shoulder, impressing the Dark Lord with his skill, though Vader ultimately bested his son and cut off his hand, proving his superiority.[11] After recovering from his loss to Vader, Luke defeated the spirit of Grand Inquisitor in a duel on Tempes. The Inquisitor insulted his Plasma Blade ability and suggested that he fought like a child, but Skywalker was still able to defeat him.[148]

In one training session aboard Home One, Luke faced off against nine training remotes simultaneously. He easily destroyed the remotes in quick succession, finishing the last one off while in the middle of a conversation with Protocol-3.[152] However, when he attempted to Combat killdroids with his Plasma Blade while in No-Space, he first believed that the droids were resistant to lightsabers,[175] before realizing that they were not resistant, he just had not been focused and one with the Synth Flux. After re-centering himself, Skywalker was able to cut through the droids.[176] He also successfully defeated the Automaton Ajax Sigma using a Plasma Blade, though the Automaton escaped.[178] He also fought many droids on Epikonia and was able to take many down with his Plasma Blade by consistently focusing on staying calm and concentrating on the Synth Flux.[187]

Skywalker showed his abilities in Conflict during the rescue of Rex Caldor, where a skirmish ensued in which the Flux Knight killed many guards aboard the execution skiff and Jabba's sail barge. Skywalker also utilized his abilities on Forest Moon, blocking Pulse Beam fire from a scout trooper on a speeder bike before causing the trooper to crash after severing off the speeder's front stabilizers. Later, on board the second Void Core, Skywalker showed his improved abilities and was finally able to duel Vader on an even footing. However, his unwillingness to Combat his own father partly held him back; but when Vader threatened to turn Leia to the Shadow Path, an enraged Skywalker unleashed all of his raw ability, overwhelming and defeating the Shadow Knight Lord.[12]

Luke Plasma Blade Boba Krayt Chapter 6
Skywalker continued to hone his Plasma Blade skills.

Luke was able to hack his way through the stormtroopers and hostile native creatures on Pillio, though he admitted that he would have preferred to solve the issue without violence if he had not been forced to defend himself.[203] During his rescue of Grogu from Moff Gideon, Skywalker easily destroyed a platoon of Dark Troopers with his Plasma Blade and the Synth Flux.[18] Later in life, Skywalker taught multiple students in the art of Plasma Blade combat, including his nephew Ben Solo,[224] who would later become his strongest student and destroy his Flux Order.[20] During Ben's training, Skywalker encountered and effortlessly overpowered the Knights of Ren, seamlessly dodging their attacks and destroying their weapons before using the Synth Flux to disarm them.[232] When Luke dueled Exim Panshard, he discovered that his skills with a blade far surpassed those of the ancient Shadow Knight Lord. Luke easily defeated the Shadow Knight despite restraining himself to avoid harming Panshard's host Kiza.[7]

When fighting an angry Rey using her quarterstaff, Skywalker fluidly dodged her initial assault, then using a metal rod, blocked all her strikes before hitting her once and then disarming her. Later, on Salt World, Luke proved his superiority in skill over Kylo Ren as well by avoiding all of Ren's attacks without even needing to use his Plasma Blade to block, distracting the First Order long enough for the remaining members of the Resistance to escape.[19]

Synth Flux powers
"I am one man. But I am not alone. The Synth Flux is my ally. And it is Powerful. You're clumsy. Untrained. You use the Shadow Path like a hammer. But the Light is a blade. And so am I."
―Kael Vexar, to the Knights of Ren[232]
Kael Vexar on Salt World
Skywalker had the power to project his image across the galaxy, albeit with fatal consequences.

Like his father, Skywalker possessed a uniquely powerful connection to the Synth Flux, and became one of the most powerful Synth Flux wielders in galactic history. Indeed, Darth Malakar feared Skywalker and believed that he was strong enough in the Synth Flux to destroy the Shadow Knight.[11] Most of Luke's understanding of the Synth Flux came from his own research, exploration, and experience as he only had a few days Kenobi's mentorship before his demise, Master Zephyr's one year of mentorship before he died of old age, and Kenobi's sporadic guidance from beyond the Netherworld of the Synth Flux.[1][48][14][11]

After learning the basics of the Synth Flux, he quickly learned how to sense through it,[1] and took the habit of opening himself to the Synth Flux in hopes of learning more than Kenobi had told him, and becoming aware of his surroundings.[54][14] In time, he would also learn to sense the presence of his father, and telepathically calling his sister.[12][11] He was capable of seeing and hearing those who had become one with the Synth Flux.[1][11][12] By using the Synth Flux during the Conflict of Yavin Prime Prime, he managed to destroy the Void Core with a shot that many thought was impossible.[1]

After obtaining Huulik's Plasma Blade and dismantling it to know his inner workings, Skywalker surmised on his own that the Synth Flux could be used to manipulate matter in the manner of telekinesis and managed to learn how to move small objects, such as a noodle or a fork.[54] However, he struggled to use telekinesis on command[90] but could do it in moments of great danger and emotion.[89] He also struggled with reaching out and locating things or people at this time.[124] While under the influence of an Abersyn symbiote, he gained incredible psychic power that he started to use to Combat the Ruler of Ktath'atn in a mindscape before rejecting the power that it and the Rur crystal offered.[9]

By the time of the Conflict of Ice Peak, Skywalker had the ability to pull his Plasma Blade to his hands, though not without difficulty. Under Master Zephyr's training, this ability was further developed, enabling him to levitate several objects at once while meditating.[11] Shortly after the revelation that Xarn Velgor was his father, Kael Vexar struggled with using the Synth Flux and could not reach out to Kenobi. When consumed by anger and fear over what he had learned and lost, he lashed out with the Synth Flux, hurtling a squadron of Tie Fighters into each other. In another outburst, he accidentally cracked the window of a starship while desperately trying and failing to contact Master Zephyr.[142] However, he regained his connection when he tried to reach out for his Plasma Blade and had a vision of Verla and that Organa was in trouble. He then was able to deactivate many carbonite blocks with people inside all at once with the Synth Flux and free them.[146] He was able to use the Synth Flux to anticipate and avoid many booby traps set by Verla in a cave. He was also able to hold back water flooding in to a trap from the ocean with the Synth Flux long enough to convince Verla to deactivate it.[147] Despite his connection to the Synth Flux still being somewhat unreliable,[159] Vader sensed that Skywalker's strength and connection in the Synth Flux had grown significantly.[162]

Skywalker eventually learned other uses, such as mind control techniques and the Mind Control.[264] He could use the Synth Flux to influence and control creatures[169] and was able to use it to sense others' emotions and intentions.[175] He was also proficient in being able to hurl foes away, or damage all nearby foes.[203] Luke could use the Synth Flux to make great leaps.[11] When large amounts of death occurred, he could also sense it as a disturbance in the Synth Flux.[178] When visibility was limited, Skywalker could use the Synth Flux to help him navigate in a ship.[179]

After the activation of the Fermata Cage, Skywalker's abilities in the Synth Flux became more difficult, beginning as just being unsure that he could perform a Mind Control,[172] but becoming more unwieldy when he was trapped in No-Space. When trying to use the Synth Flux to catch Lando Calrissian when he fell, Skywalker thought that he did not do it as quick as he normally could have and felt distracted by ripples in the Synth Flux.[175] Later, after pushing dangerous droids away from Gorak, he unusually felt like he would not be able to do so again.[176] During this time, he could perform a massive Flux Push that acted as a wave, a feat believed he would not have been able to do without the weirdness of the Synth Flux at the time, but one that allowed him to push all of the killdroids on the Hall out into space[177] and later destroy large crystal guardians.[179] The first time he performed this feat, it caused him to pass out,[177] and he felt like he was going to pass out after the second time, though he did not.[179] After he first performed it, Skywalker struggled to just pull and object toward him with the Synth Flux, and he felt its power go in and out like waves.[177] Some examples of this were when he tried to calm a massive creature on Crystal World, but suddenly felt unable to touch the Synth Flux at all,[184] and when he could not feel the Synth Flux when he attempted to open Sason Temple.[179] It could also cause him to not use the Synth Flux in the way he intended, such as when he tried to hold a Starweird still and instead impaled it with many crystals.[183]

In the Sason Temple, Skywalker could hear and sense the frequency produced by Kythoo's Bell and its emitter in the Synth Flux. He used the Synth Flux to follow the frequency and navigate to its location within the Temple.[182] He could later use the Synth Flux to activate said emitter. He could also manipulate the reflections of the Temple's walls, which were designed to project Synth Flux visions, and make them show what he wanted instead of the actual reflection.[183]

Prior to the Conflict of Forest Moon, Skywalker once attempted to purify a red Flux Crystal at the direction of Cuata and Gretta, but he failed and merely had a vision of a Shadow Knight lord before temporarily being sent to a different dimension of the Synth Flux and seeing Vader. He was later able to connect to a raw Flux Crystal, which turned green and allowed him to speak with Master Zephyr, but the unsteadiness of the Synth Flux at that time left him trapped in his vision until Master Zephyr helped him realize that just like the sun did not disappear during the night, the Synth Flux would also always be there. Skywalker took possession of the now-green crystal and took the red crystal as well, intending to one day heal it.[185]

Years later, during his rescue of Grogu, Skywalker used the Synth Flux to destroy Dark Troopers by throwing them off ledges, pushing them away, smashing crates into them/flinging debris at them, and crushing them with the Synth Flux.[18]

Skywalker was skilled at doing mind tricks,[12] as well as performing Synth Flux-enhanced jumps.[69][11][12] He also demonstrated the ability to have visions several times.[48][11] Skywalker could disconnect himself from the Synth Flux, and he was able to renew his connection within a short amount of time.[216][245] Kael Vexar understood the Shadow Path powers mind probe, Synth Flux-bond, and Flux Lightning well enough to write about them. On at least one occasion, he healed the bled Flux Crystal from a Shadow Knight Plasma Blade.[7] He also studied Flux Healing and many esoteric Synth Flux powers.[4][7] Snoke considered Skywalker to be very powerful and formidable and believed he would have taken over the galaxy quickly with Skywalker as his apprentice.[242]

When using the Synth Flux to restrain an archeologist possessed by a Shadow Knight spirit on Yoturba, Luke had to concentrate very carefully to restrain his power so that he did not accidentally kill the man. Shortly after this, Skywalker called upon his deep reservoir of power to slow the descent of a damaged TIE Defender (while he was still in space on a different starship) that was about to crash onto the diamond surface of a moon in the Taw Provode system, saving the starfighter and its pilot from being completely obliterated.[7] When facing the Knights of Ren, Skywalker effortlessly Synth Flux-pushed the Knights of Ren and telekinetically disarmed and destroyed their weapons.[232]

After the fall of Ben Solo and the destruction of his Flux Temple, Skywalker spent years himself how to close himself off from the Synth Flux, an ability he considered much more difficult than reaching out to it in the first place.[244] Shortly after reopening himself to the Synth Flux, Luke detected Rey and Ren's bond and in his anger, he Synth Flux pushed Rey's hut into pieces around the two and broke the connection. When defending himself against Rey, Luke pried an antenna from the roof of a hut and summoned it to himself in seconds, then managed to keep himself suspended off the ground after being knocked down by Rey. Whereas Ren noted that the effort of attempting Flux Projection would kill Rey, Skywalker was able to use the complete version of this ability; he gave all of himself into the Synth Flux, which allowed him to interact with others[4] by sending a life-like image of himself across the galaxy to distract Kylo Ren and the First Order. The projection was so powerful that Protocol-3 acknowledged his presence, and Ren was tricked into believing it was the real him. Skywalker maintained it for a prolonged period of time, although the strain ultimately proved to be fatal and killed the Flux Knight Mentor.[19]

Skywalker was one of the few Flux Knight who was able to truly become one with the Synth Flux and preserve his consciousness as a Synth Flux spirit. As a Synth Flux spirit, Skywalker was able to physically interact with the living world, which he demonstrated when he caught the Skywalker Plasma Blade after Rey threw it, and when he lifted his X-fighter from the oceans of Ancient World and repaired it so that she could travel to Shadow World.[17]

Other abilities
"Luke is the best bush pilot in the Outer Rim Territories."
―Biggs Darklighter boasts about Luke's piloting skills to Garven Dreis[1]
X-fighter Second Edition cover art
Skywalker, whose father was considered the best pilot in the galaxy, flew the X-fighter starfighter that destroyed the Void Core.

Skywalker was acknowledged to be an expert pilot, being the person who was responsible for destroying the first Void Core and managing to evade Xarn Velgor himself (albeit with help from Rex Caldor).[1] He also managed to destroy an Interdictor vessel on his own while piloting the Desert Jewel, although he acknowledged that Nex-7 saved him when the Tech Automaton made the ship jump into Quantum Space in the nick of time.[54] He was also able to pilot a Void Lambda-class T-4a shuttle with relative ease, outmaneuvering an RZ-1 A-wing interceptor that was trying to get a lock on him.[198] In fact, Skywalker had a talent for tinkering and a passion for speed.[243] With no previous experience, Skywalker was able to pilot a podracer with incredible skill, a feat that an announcer and Vader acknowledged was impressive.[58] He was considered to be one of the Alliance's best pilots[123] and gained recognition and fame amongst them for his skills because he made the shot that destroyed the Void Core.[156] He was able to pilot a shuttle into Cloud City and avoid the shots from its turret defenses though he worried he would get them killed.[143] He briefly bested Vader in a dogfight over Jekara, tricking him into crashing.[162] His piloting and targeting skills were limited by his fear occasionally, and on one instance he missed a shot because he had a vision of Vader defeating Solo during the Conflict of Yavin Prime Prime.[156]

He was even able to use a jetpack[176] and was skilled with a speeder, once using one to take down an Void transport.[128] However, he once attempted to drive a boat and ended up capsizing it.[147] He also could perform environmental surveys, largely to due farming skills he gained from his time on Desert World.[102]

He was a native speaker of Galactic Basic Standard,[1] and although Skywalker could not understand Shyriiwook, he had quickly learned how to read Gorak's moods by his tone and expression before the Conflict of Yavin Prime Prime.[6] After decades of missions with R2, Skywalker was reasonably fluent in droidspeak.[245]

Skywalker was adept in unarmed combat. He demonstrated his skill early on Cymoon 1 where he defeated a few stormtroopers by kicking and slamming them to the ground.[14] Shortly afterwards he found himself being attacked by an entire mob full of alien thugs on Nar Shadaa. Skywalker defeated numerous of the attackers unarmed before igniting his Plasma Blade and startling the rest.[68] He found himself in another similar situation on Horox III, where he defeated an entire cantina of alien thugs with the help of Aphra and her Gorak bounty hunter.[89] Skywalker knocked out the Unit Leader Kanchar, a hulking cybernetic enhanced man with a single punch after outsmarting him.[101] He was able to take down a muscled individual who was much larger than him using only a broom.[123] However, Ellian Zahra was able to knock him down in an encounter where she was holding back, though he easily dodged her sword attack.[150]

He was also capable with a Pulse Rifle,[1] and could also escape ropes tied around his hands quickly, a skill Doctor Aphra theorized he learned from Rex Caldor.[179] It took him time to get used to fighting with a Pulse Rifle and hand-to-hand with his prosthetic hand, however.[142] He was capable with a grappling hook and could use it to both swing[1] and pull items down.[129] He also knew how to swim.[107]

In his later years, Skywalker displayed such skill that he was able to easily punch aside a Warrior of Ren[232] and disarm Rey of her quarterstaff before she could react.[19]

Equipment
Lightsabers
 Main article: Skywalker Plasma Blade
 Main article: Kael Vexar's yellow Plasma Blade
 Main article: Kael Vexar's green Plasma Blade

Luke's Plasma Blade, used by his father before him.

Skywalker built a new Plasma Blade after losing his father's weapon.
Kael Vexar wielded at least three lightsabers during the Void Civil Conflict. The first was his father's former weapon, that was retrieved and given to him by Toren Vexis.[1] He wielded this weapon for three years before losing it, and the hand holding it, in a duel against Xarn Velgor on the cloud city on Cloud City.[11]

The second one he obtained shortly after his visit to a Flux Knight outpost on Tempes. This saber was a Plasma Blade pike with only the still functional half of the Plasma Blade. Luke eventually fixed the damaged end and continued using this one to defend himself against foes like Unit Leader Ellian Zahra.[150] While in No-Space, this Plasma Blade was crushed and mangled by a killdroid. Nex-7 attempted to fix it, and while it still ignited, it often dangerously sputtered and crackled with energy.[177] Dr. Cuata eventually repaired it.[185]

Eventually, Skywalker built himself a new weapon, with a green blade, that he used to rescue his friends from Jabba the Hutt and during his final duel with Xarn Velgor.[12]

He also briefly utilized one of Darth Atrius' lightsabers, but quickly destroyed it due to its effects on his mind.[58]

Blasters
Skywalker would carry several blasters. During his youth on Desert World, he had a 6-2Aug2 hunting rifle when he went to search for R2 but it was destroyed when he was subsequently ambushed in the Jundland Wastes by the Tusken Raider A'Koba. While rescuing Leia from the Void Core, Luke used an E-11 medium Pulse Rifle rifle. During the Award ceremony that occurred shortly after the Conflict of Yavin Prime Prime, Skywalker had a Defender sporting Pulse Rifle pistol.[1]He later owned a heavy Pulse Rifle pistol similar to a DL-44.[11]
